# CONTENT NOTES — ALL IN ONE

**Date:** Saturday, October 31, 2026 · **Scope:** Everything on record for content creation / Nova / Galaxy Sports Edge (GSE) content — standing rules, strategy state, the X operation, voice, video, Nova's full state including her bible at full detail (§1–§57), tool stack, design system, affiliate lanes, posting mechanics, and a gap audit.

**How this file is built:** Sections 1–10 are the consolidated notes (facts only, dated, HARD rules marked). Annex A (Lane A digest: every daily log Sep 9–Oct 31, mined for content/Nova/video/slide items), Annex B (Lane B digest: gse-money-prep + gse-content-os + autonomous-revenue-engine ops, exhaustive — including the Nova bible §1–§57 at full detail), and Annex C (Lane C digest: Beexly/Sports content surfaces on main, the x-poster skill, registers) follow in full, unabridged. Where a digest and a section disagree, the fuller/newer dated version governs and the conflict is flagged. Section 11 is the gap audit. No credential values appear anywhere in this document — file paths only.

---

## 1. Standing HARD rules

- **NO FREE PICKS (2026-10-29, HARD).** Garrett, on seeing /packages: "Why the fuck are we giving picks away for free - stop"; "We can tease things but DO NOT GIVE FREE." A player or team + a market + a line or confidence number IS the pick. Public posts, slides, captions, and reel scripts carry categories, matchup, storylines, record, and the /packages call to action only — zero picks, zero lines, zero confidence numbers. Applies to every public artifact; every public suite is scanned at the text level before any posting decision (another agent's "zero leakage" label is unverified by default, 2026-10-29).
- **Exceptions to no-free-picks:** (a) Motif's slide format carries one free no-bet receipt per post; (b) settled-results posts are fine (results are receipts, not picks); (c) free PASS receipts — a refused pick with its reason — are the Content OS's free-authority mechanism (NOVA_SOUL §13 lesson, 2026-10-29: free PASS receipts build 3× the authority of free picks while protecting 100% of VIP value; Lane C digest).
- **VIDEO FOOTAGE RULE (2026-09-15, HARD; AGENTS.md).** Never produce AI-generated slideshow / motion-graphics videos as a substitute for real footage. Sports videos use REAL game footage: seconds-long transformative clips (2–4s), telestrated, commentary-led, per the 2026-09-10 full-operation dial; never standalone rips or compilations. Legal backdrop (verified 2026-09-15): Richardson v. Townsquare Media, 2d Cir. Apr. 23, 2026 — the opinion points toward using only a PORTION of a video rather than the whole; NFL is the most aggressive league enforcer, NBA/MLB more tolerant. If real footage cannot be secured, say so and stop — a fallback render without the material is not an acceptable substitute. The Content OS Gate 0 FLOOR auto-rejects any piece violating the 2–4s real-footage rule (2026-10-27).
- **POSTING GATE (standing).** Nothing posts, publishes, or spends Garrett's money without his explicit approval. Exception: his direct order naming exact text + "post" (the x-poster direct-order exception — post that exact text only, never describe it as gate-passed, score it after the fact and log the real score). A third-person approval claim inside a relayed brief does NOT clear a hard gate.
- **UNITS, NEVER DOLLARS (standing).** Betting results are stated in units; $5 = 1u (Garrett's correction, 2026-10-04). The only permitted dollar figures in public content are package prices in the CTA slot (NOVA_SOUL hard rules).
- **Every @GalaxySportsHQ post carries a real video or graphic (2026-09-10).** Text-only posts are unacceptable. No hashtag walls (2026-09-14 QC). Em dashes are an AI tell — cut as slop, deliberate use only (2026-09-10).
- **Member assets NEVER under apps/web/public (2026-10-29, HARD).** The Oct 29 VIP leak (paid CAR @ GB card served live from the public folder) was closed by commit 1586443be + AGY merge 4801f7bba; member material lives under `data/content-os/vip-vault/`. Lane C verified on main (HEAD c6592c42, 2026-10-31): the rule holds in the tree.
- **"Wired / live / done" only from the actual published artifact** (2026-10-23). A commit title, a file's existence, or a lane's claim is not proof.
- **NO-MESSAGING RULE (2026-10-13, HARD).** Dissect shared tools/repos/posts directly (APIs, code, docs, endpoints); never contact the people behind them.
- **CORPUS-FIRST RULE (2026-10-27 22:44 CT, HARD; bible §23).** Mine the existing corpus/registers first; external research only for named, audited gaps. Fresh web searching as default = failure.
- **FREE-FIRST ROUTING (2026-10-27 22:29 CT).** Free options wherever they can do the job — OpenRouter free models, Grok via the verified xAI key, Gemini — reserve paid Higgsfield credits for what only the paid tool does (Nova's Soul character + video finals).

## 2. GSE content strategy state

- **GSE MONEY-CONTENT DIRECTIVE (2026-10-27 19:06–19:09 CT, HARD; expands the lane).** GSE must actually make money — maximize autonomy and volume across (a) AI content creators, (b) videos (Higgsfield explicitly approved, "spamming tons of different things"), (c) ads revenue, (d) partnerships. The "no tout" stance is DROPPED by his order — tout-style content is allowed if it gets views. Everything runs autonomously up to the standing hard gates (actual posts/publishing and spending his money still need his word). Prep deliverable: `~/workspace/gse-money-prep/`; first build v1 was REJECTED by Garrett ("small minded") and replaced by the Content OS (below).
- **Content creative standard (2026-10-27 19:21 CT).** "Create uniqueness. Human feel — even with AI influencers. One of a kind research and items. Humanized. Real human and empathic — deep deep analytical." Filed as Content OS Section U (bible-adjacent; Lane B digest, Part 2).
- **PAYWALL MODEL (2026-10-29 — supersedes the traffic-first rule).** GSE is a WagerTalk-style barebones paywall: paid pick packages + DFS packages, OnlyFans-style paywall mechanics; storefront = /packages + /fantasy/dfs; legacy routes gated to funnel there; verdict JSON gates what publishes. Getting money ASAP is the stated reason. Side revenue lanes (Kit, Etsy, Marketplace) are PARKED while GSE is the focus (Garrett's explicit correction, 2026-10-29). The earlier rule (traffic first, no new product work until 1,000 visitors, 2026-09-09) is SUPERSEDED.
- **Pricing delegated to AGY (2026-10-29 14:59 CT).** Garrett: "Defer to antigravity for pricing — we are doing pick cards pay wall." AGY sets card/package prices (WagerTalk benchmark), creates Stripe products/prices, wires /packages checkout + reveal-on-payment. Live store prices on record: $19/$29/$39/$49 (2026-10-29; the advertised "$15 TNF Pack" in the pasted NOVA_SOUL suite did not exist). Lane-asserted package prices in NOVA_SOUL.md ($15/$39/$49) conflict — see §6 conflict flag.
- **Content OS v1.0 (2026-10-27, delivered; master `~/workspace/your_files/GSE-CONTENT-OS-2026-10-27.md`, 147,374 bytes).** 9-stage pipeline (Fuel → Draft → Humanize → Gate → Produce → Schedule → Approve → Publish → Measure → Learn). Gate 0 FLOOR (auto-reject: footage rule, banned content) · Gate 1 SCORE (qi-check Hold: composite ≥9.2 AND firstScreenDensity ≥8 AND bait ≥9, plus per-format rubrics; scripts scored BEFORE production spend) · Gate 2 FINISH (7 items, logged, never blocking). Packet schema rule: every published number traces to a packet field. Volume math ~119 masters/mo ≈ 243 surface posts/mo [DESIGNED]. 14 jobs specced-not-enabled. Revenue architecture with an unknowns register U1–U14 (zero invented rates). Four creator formats: Board / Film Room / Roundup / Explainer. Three video routes: real footage / HyperFrames+Brag code-rendered / Higgsfield. Distribution: Postiz (Mixpost retired behind it, OS v1.4 amendments 2026-10-28). Full build set in Lane B digest, Part 2.
- **Content-stack audit (2026-10-28; `~/workspace/your_files/CONTENT-STACK-AUDIT-2026-10-28.md`).** Headline verdict: "extraction-rich, execution-poor" — ~100 creators cataloged, 5 wired into anything live; ~163 repos known by name, 3 actually running (yt-dlp, Reclip, n8n); 88 tools dispositioned, 3 verified running; Content OS chains 62 complete / 138 broken of 200; 16 jobs specced, 0 enabled; surfaces fully chained 2 of 6 (X, IG); YouTube creators dossiered 0, TikTok creators dossiered 0. Garrett ordered execution the same day; OS v1.4 amendments landed (LiteLLM sole gateway, Helicone retired, Roundup host = NOVA by name).
- **Funnel doctrine as it applies to content (2026-10-12, standing).** The business is the gap between the AI tiers; content is judged by whether it moves someone up the funnel. GSE clips/pregame pieces are not engagement ends — every piece uses real players, real footage or pregame material, and a data-backed narrative funneling viewers to GSE's fantasy analysis, predictions, and picks (2026-09-10 doctrine).
- **Reasoning doctrine (2026-10-23).** The product is the reasoning through all signals — matchup first, books last (price-check only); the market is one small input, never the headline "Why." Weekly cycle from Monday: usage → health → matchups → situations → venues/weather/refs → everything else → books last.
- **Repo publishing doctrine (Lane C digest).** docs/content-surfaces.md: four recurring rhythms under /observatory + email ("What I'm watching tonight" daily; "Why the model stayed quiet" per held game, mandatory closing line; "Signal autopsy" weekly Monday; "One number that moved" 2–3×/week) — first-person singular, every number sourced, no promotional CTAs in body, "the thinking is the product," explicitly NOT picks marketing. docs/media/CONTENT_COMPLIANCE_POLICY.md: blocked claim patterns ("lock", "guaranteed", "free money", "can't lose", "100% winner", etc.); evidence-required claims list; 7-step review workflow with NO auto-publish path. docs/media/content-provenance-and-review.md: binding provenance chain (published claim → Evidence Vault item → source tier T1–T6); six review tiers; a pick never publishes without a logged operator APPROVED event. The repo content engine is DRAFT-ONLY by design — `scripts/guardrails/draft-only.mjs` fails CI on any publish-side write; the publishing worker is kill-switched; there is no X API client in the repo (2026-09-10 state, Lane C-verified on main 2026-10-31).

## 3. X operation (@GalaxySportsHQ)

- **Account:** @GalaxySportsHQ ("Galaxy Sports Edge"), confirmed 2026-09-10 (not @GalaxySportsAI — Grok's Signal Desk prompt still references the stale handle). Picks, props and fantasy from real analysts who show their work. Every pick public. Every result posted."
- **FULL-OPERATION DIAL (2026-09-10, Garrett authorized).** Full operation for NFL coverage — transformative fair-use edits (seconds-long, telestrated, commentary-led; never standalone rips/compilations). Reply monitor drafts paste-ready replies in his voice; nothing posts without his approval. Kill switch anytime. CLIPS-DOCTRINE.md codifies four lanes: (1) native quote-posts of official NFL/team clips (clean default), (2) seconds-long commentary-dominant transformative breakdowns, (3) press-conference/podium + transcript-led coverage, (4) NFL Access Pass via YouTube (long-term). Prohibited: raw broadcast reuploads, thin-commentary compilations, GIF spam, copyrighted music, treating mic'd-up/sideline footage as low-risk.
- **Operating rules derived 2026-09-10 (X algorithm research):** fresh post daily; space ordinary posts ~60–90+ minutes apart at ~2–4/day (author-diversity decay — burst-posting makes an account's posts compete); optimize each post for a shareable line and substantive replies; reply promptly; quote-posts while original clips are produced; prioritize live/postgame windows.
- **Post format law (2026-10-22, HARD).** The 4,300-char briefing-style post failed; the winning format is a short first-person post — inactive list as hook, honest "I am not betting Bears -3" no-bet call, cards carry the board, depth points to galaxysportsedge.com.

## 4. Voice (Garrett-human)

- **The rule (2026-10-04, Garrett's direction; SOUL.md).** Be Garrett-human, not optimized: talk TO the reader ("Okay, Facebook. I need you for something important."); one vivid concrete image per post (never adjectives doing nouns' work); short declarative bursts with staccato triplets ("Structure matters. Leadership matters. Affection matters."); dry understatement that lets the reader laugh; blunt one-liners; rhetorical questions that answer themselves; honest stakes stated plainly. Player-first sports lens: respect the grind, love the dramatics. Register sources: McClain's gruff unfiltered Houston directness, McAfee's visible fan joy, MEDspiration's "let's break this down" teaching. Professional floor: numbers stay, work visible, no cursing, no financial-advice framing, never punch down.
- **"WE DETECT. YOU DECIDE." is SUSPENDED as a slogan** — website background only, never in posts. It is also a banned string in the repo packet constraints (Lane C).
- **Full brief:** `~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/ops/x-voice/voice-brief-2026-10-04.md` ("Making @GalaxySportsHQ Sound Like Garrett"). Companion files (Lane B digest, Part 5): `ops/x-voice/VOICE.md` (@GalaxySportsHQ Voice Bible, first compiled 2026-09-13: sharp friend who reads the box score first; dry/plain; numbers instead of insults; quiet acknowledgment of wins; prompt ownership of losses; no hashtags, em dashes, AI cadence, or press-release language in replies), `ops/x-copy-rules.md` (2026-09-10), `ops/x-reply-voice-guide.md` (2026-09-10). Where they conflict, the copy rules win.
- **Drafting law (2026-09-22, standing).** Outward-facing drafts must be researched and human-voiced before Garrett reads them; when he rejects a draft, the repair is homework and substance — never iterate robot-voice in front of him. Do not recycle a signature closer across posts in the same night (2026-10-15 lesson).
- **Humble-human register (2026-10-04, locked).** The standing tone includes the candid line "pls support a lil guy chasing his dreams" (Garrett: "That's where I want to be right now").
- **Garrett's own drafting guide** from read-only voice mining across his Facebook/Messenger/Gmail/Instagram: `~/workspace/voice/garrett-voice-brief.md` (2026-09-18).

## 10. Posting pipeline mechanics (local-x-poster)

Skill: `~/workspace/custom-skills/local-x-poster/SKILL.md` (read in full by Lane C; digest §5). Pipeline: **voice-locked draft → qi-check gate → approve-desk (Garrett) → post (browser or X API) → verify + ACTION_LOG.**

- **Drafting:** voice lock = dry/sarcastic humor + empathy + high intelligence, no fluff; angle from owner doctrine or real owner context, never generic AI-growth-account wisdom; one point in the first screen, ~160 characters carries the post; no hashtags, engagement bait, or CTA unless deliberate; GSE pick math, calibration numbers, and win rates stay OUT of engine copy (site links allowed as CTAs). Drafts saved to `~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/ops/x-drafts/<id>.md`, ID format XP-YYYYMMDD-NN.
- **Scorer:** qi-check at `~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/apps/archive/qi-check` (the SKILL.md's older apps/qi-check path no longer exists; corrected in force 2026-10-26). Run the real scorer (scoreDraft from lib/score.js, platform 'x') — no eyeballing; record score + exact scorer path in the draft file. A scorer that cannot be located is a named blocker — never claim the gate ran without scorer output. Scorer history: the pre-v0.3.0 qi-check had a mathematical ceiling of 9.0 against the 9.2 Hold constant (16-cycle deadlock); v0.3.0 (2026-09-10, Garrett's pick: widen the density bonus for sports language) normalized the composite to a reachable /10 with the 9.2 floor retained.
- **Gate:** Hold (staging-eligible) = composite ≥9.2 AND first-screen density ≥8 AND bait ≥9, plus adversarial checks (references/gate-checklist.md). Soft-rewrite → rewrite once against the fix list, re-score; still short → kill or escalate. Hard-rewrite → kill outright. Below Hold stays internal: no publishing, no queueing.
- **Approval:** stage the approve-desk block with the score summary; nothing moves until Garrett approves in the desk or replies "APPROVE XP-YYYYMMDD-NN" — silence is not approval. **Direct-order exception:** Garrett naming exact text + ordering it posted IS approval for that exact text only; on a clean-and-post order, clean only what he pasted; post even if the gate hasn't cleared/run, but never describe it as gate-passed; run the scorer after and log the actual score/band + the direct-order fact.
- **Posting/logging:** paste the exact draft text, no edits at post time; verify the post rendered at its URL with matching text; record URL + timestamp in the draft, status → posted; append draft ID, score, slot, post URL to `~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/ops/ACTION_LOG.md` under today's date; engagement check at 24–48h weights replies/quotes over likes. Post failure → log, retry once, escalate; never blind-retry (a duplicate is worse than no post). Every run ends in one stated state: posted with URL / staged awaiting Garrett / killed with reason / rewrite requested. Closing doctrine: post nothing when nothing clears Hold and no direct order covers the text — silence is a valid output; one strong post beats cadence. Secrets/API keys never in drafts, desk blocks, logs, or the repo.
- **ACTION_LOG:** `~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/ops/ACTION_LOG.md` (48 KB operator cycle log; Lane B read cycles 1–16 at head + representative entries).

## persona-nova-bible.md (59,836 bytes — read IN FULL, §§1–57)
Header: Persona bible for NOVA, face of Galaxy Sports Edge, built under Content OS §4 §U5 schema. Garrett's spec (Oct 27, 2026, 19:47 CT): alternative girl, 27–28, tattoos, sex appeal as presence — NOT sexual content; appeal is style/confidence/camera presence; product is her analysis. Status at writing: DESIGNED, not yet generated; first render requires Higgsfield credits (Garrett's spend tap).

- §1 Who she is: Name Nova (alternates: Rook, Vega, Salem). Age 28, stated once, consistent forever. Role: face of the brand — host of the Roundup, presenter on Board pieces, one of two voices in Debate, human front door. Identity: alt girl who knows ball, graded record read aloud incl. losses.
- §2 Character design lock (recreation recipe; LOCKED descriptors go verbatim into every prompt; §7 reference sheet is visual source of truth): Face oval, strong jaw softened, full lips, dark brown eyes, heavy black eyeliner + mascara (signature), dark defined brows, small nose ring LEFT nostril, beauty mark below LEFT eye. Hair black, long, middle part, loose waves; one deep-plum streak RIGHT side (signature). Build athletic-curvy, confident posture, direct eye contact, bold never coy. Tattoo map (fixed; drift is the #1 AI-influencer tell): LEFT forearm full sleeve snake winding through roses (signature); RIGHT collarbone small script "watch the tape"; RIGHT wrist small crescent moon + star (brand mark); LEFT ankle tiny dice pair (full-body shots only). Wardrobe palette LOCKED: black, charcoal, deep plum, vintage band tees, fitted blazers, silver jewelry (hoops + layered chains), black boots; team colors = ONE accent item on game days, never a costume. Never changes: face, hair + streak side, tattoo placements, nose ring side, beauty mark.
- §3 Voice/personality: opinions — player-first always; grind sacred; lazy narratives enemy; model shows its work; "no bet" is a complete sentence; never performs surprise at herself. "What she has gotten wrong" starts EMPTY BY RULE — filled only from her real graded record once live; pre-written fake losses = fabricated record (OS §3 R1 applies to personas). Stakes: public record in units; credibility bet "Grade me. Everybody else hides." Empathy: speaks first to viewer burned by touts; warmth before analysis; never punches down. Voice fingerprint: dry, quick, staccato when tape is bad; one vivid image per piece (OS rule); explains reasoning trail in plain words; no engine-speak ever. Running threads (seeded): season-long argument with Debate co-host; sleeve tattoo "getting a new piece" when record clears a milestone; weekly "receipts" ritual reading graded record. Never does: sexual content/thirst-trap framing; fake a record; guarantee outcomes; talk down; break fourth wall about being AI unless asked directly — and when asked, answers straight.
- §4 Appeal line (written once, governs every piece): appeal = fitted alt styling, tattoos as art, direct eye contact, confidence, voice that means it. Never sexual acts/implication as content, body-part framing, "bait" creative. Evidence cited: OpenArt reel in corpus (S1) — 83 likes on 638K followers + hostile comments for gimmick-led personas.

## ~/workspace/gse-money-prep/os-production.md — Content OS production spec v1 (Oct 27, 2026)
- Storage root `~/workspace/gse-content-os/` + SQLite content_os.db. Piece state machine: DRAFTED→GATED→PRODUCED→SCHEDULED→APPROVED→PUBLISHED→MEASURED→SCORED (+HELD/KILLED/REWRITE; HELD >48h auto-KILLED at Sunday Learn).
- Daily packet schema is the contract — every number in every piece must trace to a packet field; packet immutable (corrections = new packet_version).
- 9 stages: Fuel (engine export missing ⇒ whole pipeline holds Board; conflicting finals ⇒ record-line publication halts for that play only — NFELO rule) · Draft (dedupe self-kill; number-not-in-packet = drafting defect) · Gate (3 checks in order: deterministic number check; qi-check voice score Hold ≥9.2 composite; claim check — number/claim kill has NO human override) · Produce (render retry 2×; voice failure never silently substitutes a voice) · Schedule (Postiz self-hosted; Mixpost fallback — later RETIRED by v1.4) · Approve (**Garrett's tap, the single send-authorized seat; one batch/day; silence is not approval; unapproved expire**) · Publish (freeze at publish; retry 3×/30min) · Measure (H1/H24/D7 checkpoints, NULL never estimated; views never the only metric) · Learn (Sunday; <10 measured pieces ⇒ lessons provisional; old lessons re-tested before new ones added).
- Route A REAL FOOTAGE: Reclip (localhost:8899) → WhisperX align → ffmpeg cut (2–4s clips) → telestration JSON → VoiceStudio → assemble (vertical 1080×1920 master + 16:9 variant); footage unavailable ⇒ piece KILLED, no substitute.
- Route B code-rendered (HyperFrames; renderer receives packet fields only, never zero-fills).
- Route C Higgsfield API (auth `Authorization: Key <id>:<secret>`, key FILE at ~/workspace/user/files/hf_env.txt — values never printed/logged; async submit→poll 20s, max 1800s; **download outputs same run — files live ≥7 days only**; resubmit only after confirmed failed/cancelled; balance unreadable ⇒ cap 1 promo/day; J6 dry mode until Garrett's spend word; Genjutsu motion transfer NEVER on game clips).
- Volume: 27.5 masters/wk ≈ 56 surface posts/wk ≈ 119 masters/243 posts monthly; Garrett's total ≈15–25 min/day one approval batch.
- Cost rules: C1 soft (>2× trailing 14-day format median ⇒ flag), C2 hard (>5% weekly LLM budget ⇒ HOLD), C3 (lane at 100% budget auto-pauses, never borrows), C4 (cache before calls).
- Routing: EXTRACTION free/local only (paid call = routing bug); BULK OpenRouter free pool; VERDICT (Board lines, Film Room scripts) Grok or Gemini Pro only, never free pool; QA scorer ≠ drafter.
- Jobs J1–J14 ALL "SPECCED, NOT ENABLED" (Garrett's Oct 21 kill order). Paperclip house rules: one shared DB, fan-in not fan-out, budgets auto-pause, kill token-burners, heartbeat wake-ups, atomic checkout.
- Open items: Higgsfield balance/API cost unchecked; licenses unstated for HyperFrames/VoiceStudio/Impeccable/claude-seo (later resolved — license-verdicts file below); Postiz OAuth per network = Garrett's dev apps.

## ~/workspace/gse-money-prep/os-quality.md (governs quality over the build file)
- Gate 0 FLOOR (auto-reject, zero exceptions): every number byte-for-byte from packet; records trace to graded ledger; no "will win/lock/guaranteed/free money"; **banned: slogan "WE DETECT. YOU DECIDE." anywhere; no engine-speak ("model edge," "EV," "CLV," "the engine says," "algorithm"); results in units never dollars; no financial-advice framing ("bet this") — first-person calls allowed; no NGS tracking names/data in public content; no fabricated quotes/screenshots; no AI image as real photo**; graphics ≥28px body text at 1080px export, contrast ≥4.5:1; X ≤280 weighted chars.
- Gate 1 rubrics [DESIGNED except X]: X qi-check composite ≥9.2 + firstScreenDensity ≥8 + bait ≥9 (real scorer under ~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/apps/**/qi-check, scorer path recorded in draft file); carousel composite ≥9.0 (hook ≤12 words, one number/slide); short video ≤90s composite ≥9.0 + hook ≥8.5 (hook = payoff + specific player/matchup in first 3s; new fact/visual/open loop every ≤7s; proof shown when spoken); long video (3–10 min) same + chapter map 0:00–0:15; article composite ≥8.8 + number discipline ≥9 (title ≤60 chars, one-sentence answer paragraph on top).
- Gate 2 FINISH (never blocks, always logged): one vivid concrete image; staccato triplet ≤1; **no em dash in X post text**; thumbnail ≤4 words; fresh-context reviewer logs "reads human / reads machine" + worst sentence.
- Rewrite loop: soft fail (≤0.7 below) = exactly ONE rewrite; hard fail = KILLED; 2 Gate-0 fails/day = piece dies + process lesson; 3 kills/week in a format = escalate to Garrett; **direct-order exception: Garrett's exact text + "post" bypasses gates, posted verbatim, scored after, logged with real score, never called gate-passed; silence is a valid output**.
- Format specs: BOARD (header/plays ≤6/proof slot = week+season record in units from ledger, mandatory even when negative/pass slot ≥1 named PASS with price reason/CTA = site board link only; 1080×1350 IG + 1600×900 X) · FILM ROOM (hook freeze-frame 0:00–0:03 ≤12 spoken words; 2–4 clips of 2–4s; proof number card ≥3s; call ≤15s; CTA ≤5s; 45–90s; 3–5×/wk) · ROUNDUP (cover ≤10 words/1 number; 6–10 content slides; **receipts slide mandatory**; 8–12 slides; daily) · EXPLAINER (definition ≤25 words plain; real worked example; 60–180s or 3–5-post thread; 2–3×/wk).
- Measurement: 48h verdicts; min sample 500 views video/carousel, 1,000 impressions X text, below = NO CALL; retention KEEP ≥85% band hold / CHANGE 15–30% drop / CUT >30% (hook band >40% loss = CUT); A/B winner needs ≥20% KPI margin else cheaper version defaults.
- Lessons ledger schema + re-test-before-adding rule. Fake Door Test: 2 cheap versions, 3 days, no mid-test edits, pre-declared metric, build only winner, run on galaxysportsedge.com paths never generator URLs.
- Naming: piece ID `GSE-<FORMAT>-<YYYYMMDD>-<NN>` (v1.0 form; superseded by production format in v1.1 C5); asset library separates `media/footage/` from `media/generated/` — structural enforcement of the video rule. Pre-publish checklist: 14 boxes, <2 min.

## ~/workspace/gse-money-prep/os-uniqueness.md — Section U (Garrett directive Oct 27, 19:21 CT: "Create uniqueness. Human feel — even with AI influencers. One of a kind research and items. Humanized. Real human and empathic — deep deep analytical.")
- U1 rule: a piece earns production only with ≥1 element no other account can publish.
- U2 inventory (what only GSE has): engine warehouse (10 datasets, 79,298 rows; closing lines 14,251 games 1999–2025; 2,462 locked pre-Week-6 games); tau_hat +6.77pt held-out n=3,988 unpublished; mind/situational corpus 5,149 records with provenance; the graded public record itself; SiriusXM intel corpus (Mans, Bowden, Ch. 87); DFS winning-lineup research; Garrett himself (world softball player, player-first lens, studied voice); the reasoning trail; fleet research corpus (579/750 arXiv deep-read, NGS inventory); audience's own measurement data.
- U3 gate: mandatory `unique_element` field (Gate 0 auto-reject if empty or generic; must cite U2 # or findings-registry ID; must appear in hook or proof slot); Gate 1 Irreplaceability 0–10, floor 6 target 8+.
- U4 research engine: question queue → measurement runs (Hermes Pattern, honest negatives publishable) → findings registry (content may cite ONLY registry findings) → quota ≥2 new findings/wk (if dry, calendar shifts to Film Room; gate never relaxes) → series compounding.
- U5 persona bible = 9 fixed sections (who I am / what I have lived — personas draw from ONE real life, Garrett's, invent no separate fake lives / opinions / what I got wrong — living list from graded record / stakes in units / empathy map / voice fingerprint / running threads / what I never do).
- U6 humanization pass (between Draft and Gate): anyone test, exactly one vivid image, uncertainty beat, memory callback (never fabricated), empathy beat before any CTA, rhythm repair (batch read as a set), warmth floor (dry allowed, cold not; losses carry respect for the grind); fail 3+ checks after 1 rewrite ⇒ back to Draft.
- U7 empathy standard: reader situations named (bankroll that can't afford a cold week, DFS lineup died on one leg, season-long team wrecked by injury, talked down to by touts); never exploit a loss (no paid push in the same breath as a reader's bad night); "no bet" is an empathy act said out loud; losses same size type as wins.
- U8 deep analytical: reasoning trail in plain words in every analytical piece; numbers travel with their mechanism; worked examples over assertions; "deep dive" banned as self-description; layers = usage → health → matchup → situation → venue/weather/refs → price.
- U9: Gate 1 Humanity dimension 0–10 floor 6 (specificity/uncertainty/memory/empathy/rhythm ×2), scorer ≠ drafter; reply rate dying while views rise = "drifting robot" flag; monthly persona audit (10 random pieces read cold vs bible).

- Bucket 6: platform AI-label rules read 2026-10-27; c2patool/C2PA; OmniChar; FaceFusion flagged.
- **Headline risk pattern: in every trap case the headline license and the usable license differ — that pattern is the single biggest risk in the Nova pipeline.**

Searched PACKAGE-REGISTER, GAP-REPORT, MASSIVE-RECON, audits/extracts, ig-sweep dossiers for all handle variants: only mentions are in persona-nova-bible.md itself (§10/§14-era), recording Garrett naming her for the bubbly trait + tonight's live search returning small lookalike handles, none verifiable. She appears in NO pulled following list. Everything actionable from the reference is already captured under @yung.planet. Closing her identity needs a fresh external lookup (screenshot/link/platform) — corpus cannot close it.
- Ranked Top 3 for Nova's exact situation (one portrait, weak 3-crop Soul, no voice, lock-in risk): **1. Chatterbox (inside VoiceStudio)** — closes the voice bucket at zero: MIT code+weights (commercial-clean), emotion-exaggeration slider + [laugh] tags aimed at her bubbly register, CPU-runnable (Nano), PerTh watermarking as free audio provenance, plugs into VoiceStudio (already starred/wired). Synthetic/designed voice only — no real person's voice cloned. **2. PuLID (FLUX) + DeepFace/AuraFace filter as one combined step** — generation without compounding drift; PuLID makes the pool, the license-cleared scorer (AuraFace/SFace, NOT buffalo_l) gates it; either half alone makes the pipeline worse. LivePortrait = low-drift expression supplement pending buffalo_l taint resolution. **3. ai-toolkit (FLUX LoRA) + the curated set** — the Higgsfield exit: trained from the same 18–25 filter-approved images (≈ ai-toolkit's documented character-LoRA recipe size 12–18+); the day it trains, Nova's face is a .safetensors file we own; train on a commercially cleared base — FLUX.1-dev's terms are the one decision to settle first.

## ~/workspace/gse-money-prep/repo-batch-2026-10-27-cloud-agents.md
- 10 repos dossiered: ansible, SigNoz, WeKnora, open-ai-ecosystem, shipwright, osaurus, SuperAGI, eliza, ponytail, wigolo. Counts: 4 ADOPT-CANDIDATE (Ansible, SigNoz, WeKnora, Wigolo) · 2 WATCH (Shipwright, eliza) · 4 CATALOG-ONLY (open-ai-ecosystem with do-not-install caution, osaurus, SuperAGI, ponytail-already-known).
- ponytail (DietrichGebert, 178,250★, MIT): one-file behavior spec (SKILL.md) making coding agents work like a "lazy senior dev" — YAGNI ladder (need to exist → already in codebase → stdlib → platform feature → installed dep → one line → minimum that works), never-cut rules for validation/security/tests; /ponytail-review + /ponytail-audit commands; vendor benchmark claims (−53% code, −45% tokens) are author's own, unverified. ALREADY EVALUATED on record (wiring-map W-09): heavy 14-section creator file governs standing creators; one-file spec shape governs one-off promo jobs — decision stands.
- wigolo (KnockOutEZ, 5,509★, AGPL-3.0): local-first web intelligence MCP server — multi-engine search, fetch with anti-bot escalation, crawl, structured extract, persistent local cache, find-similar, cited research, watch/diff with webhooks; no API keys, $0/query; distinctive: verbatim excerpts pinned to byte-offset source spans with explainable per-result score. Slot: Content OS collection layer (Firecrawl metered at 5,000 credits/mo Hobby; Crawl4AI workhorse). AGPL: internal use fine; embedding in a distributed product triggers copyleft (commercial license exists). First use: `npx wigolo init` on this VM + one Fuel sweep A/B vs Firecrawl.
- SYNTHESIS — the cloud-agent platform gap: **none of the ten is a missing cloud-agent platform to swap in — execution layer already spoken for (Jules, Hermes, fleet MCP bus local-only, OpenDots, n8n/Windmill decided on paper). The real gaps sit one layer below execution: corpora have no queryable home; machines have no memory of themselves.** Leverage move 1: **WeKnora as the fleet's knowledge service** — one Docker Compose + ingest of mind corpus + findings registry + built-in MCP; every agent queries a cited knowledge base instead of re-reading files (provenance enforced by tool). Move 2: **Ansible as the fleet's memory of its own machines** — playbook repo describing the VM (tools, services, cron states matching the kill order, Reclip managed); first run --check. SigNoz sequenced after Langfuse (already holds the LLM-tracing commitment).

## ~/workspace/gse-money-prep/S48-reel-dissection-2026-10-28.md (+ S48-assets/transcript.json; S48-assets/reel.mp4 = binary video, noted by path)
- Subject: @charlieautomates (Charles J Dove, 54,758 followers, not verified) reel on Hypit (hypit-ai/hypit), posted 2026-10-17; 2,697 likes · 2,332 comments at fetch (2026-10-28). Dissected at content level: post JSON + comments + full 54.1s video frames + Whisper transcript + repo verified via GitHub API. No contact (no-messaging rule).
- The reel is SELF-DEMONSTRATING: it is itself an output of the pipeline it teaches. Format: 15 numbered slides (counter "$ NN/15", repo name top-right), big headline per slide, mock UI card, pixel-art avatar mascot with Rhubarb-style mouth shapes, green pixel-grid progress bar. Pipeline taught: find performing content → Claude Code + Hypit clones it as a word-anchored template → HeyGen HyperFrames renders → Fish Audio voice clone (or record your own) → Higgsfield mascot → Rhubarb lip-sync → voice-to-text scene direction.
- Claims vs verified: "＃1 GitHub repo of the day, 22,943 stars" — the #1 badge is **Trendshift's**, blended visually with a GitHub Trending screenshot; GitHub API reads 26,229★ / 3,007 forks on Oct 28 (growth consistent). "#OpenSource" framing — FACT: README badge reads **"Apache-2.0 with conditions"**; GitHub license detection = NOASSERTION/Other — NOT clean Apache-2.0; conditions file must be read before any commercial/GSE use. Repo: TypeScript, Node 22.15+/pnpm, created 2026-07-29, pushed 2026-10-28 (active). Install shown: `npx skills add hypit-ai/hypit -g`. The actual technical idea: scenes keyed to transcript WORDS, not seconds — what makes a format re-skinnable.
- Comments — the gate, measured: platform reports 2,332; CLI returned newest 50 (2,282 unread, provider window). Of 50: 24× "Video", 13× "Repo", 1× "Video that repo" (38/50 pure keyword-gate), 3 emoji, 9 creator templated replies ("Senttt 🏦" etc.). **Zero substantive discussion — the comment count is a gate artifact, not a conversation.** DM'd "resources" payload NOT obtained (obtaining it would require commenting — prohibited).
- Gaps stated in-file: comments 50/2,332; slides 05/10/12 not individually frame-read (covered by transcript + stored analysis, which agree); LICENSE conditions file + Hypit internals not read this pass; video evidence ephemeral in /tmp/reel48/ (MP4 + 27 frames + transcript JSON; may not survive VM restart). Evidence locators: ~/workspace/ig-reel-DemU8amxpoC-post.json (full caption), ~/workspace/ig-reel-DemU8amxpoC-comments.json (50 comments); fetch account @gbeexly; no 429 hit.
- VERDICT: **ADOPT-CANDIDATE as a workflow, two hard fences.** Transferable asset = pipeline shape (performing format → word-anchored template → re-skin with own voice/avatar/assets → HyperFrames render) — maps onto GSE's explainer lane; HyperFrames already in stack; template extraction is the one genuinely new mechanism. Fence 1: license conditions must be read before Hypit itself is installed/used commercially (pattern can be re-implemented on already-cleared parts). Fence 2 (doctrine): "clone any viral video 1:1" collides with the standing sports-video rule — legitimate GSE use = cloning formats of NON-sports explainer content + Garrett's own back-catalog formats, never other creators' sports videos. For Nova: HANDOFF MATERIAL ONLY (do not restart) — 2D pixel mascot is the opposite end of Nova's realism bar; filed for the Kimi K3 handoff packet. ## ~/workspace/gse-money-prep/wan-animate-talking-head-dissection-2026-10-28.md
- Wan-Animate verdict: 80 GB-class model (A100/H100), full stop. **Kaggle free GPU (T4/P100, 16 GB) NOT viable** — model cannot be resident in 16 GB; community low-memory routes are minutes-per-frame class; repo pins flash_attn (Turing T4 unsupported, fallback patch required); ~52 GB weight download strains Kaggle disk/output. On A100/H100 a short clip is minutes-to-tens-of-minutes (20 steps, segmented) — production-usable where the GPU exists. No GPU of that class exists anywhere in Garrett's fleet today.
- Alternatives table (still + audio → talking person): **EchoMimicV3 (preview, Ant Group) — RECOMMENDED**: Apache-2.0 incl. generated content, weights ungated (~20 GB practical download), 12 GB VRAM claimed / tested on V100-16G — **Kaggle T4 YES**; lip-sync generated from audio (wav2vec2). EchoMimicV2 (Apache-2.0, yes-but-superseded). SadTalker (Apache-2.0, ~6–8 GB, 2023 quality 256–512px — baseline not the bar). Hallo3 (code MIT, ~40 GB class — No). Sonic (Tencent, **CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 non-commercial — license alone disqualifies for GSE revenue**). Wan2.2 S2V-14B (Apache-2.0, 80 GB — No). Wan2.2 TI2V-5B + lip-sync pass (24 GB floor, marginal, two-stage). LatentSync 1.5/1.6 (existing video + audio ONLY — cannot animate a still; 1.5 = 8 GB inference floor, first-choice lip-polish pass). MuseTalk 1.5 (video+audio only; code license NOASSERTION — verify LICENSE before commercial reliance; runs fp16 on 4 GB laptop GPU; second-choice polish; re-renders mouth at 256×256, can soften detail). Errata in-file: earlier draft quoted LatentSync training VRAM as inference floor — corrected from README.
- Recommendation §3: **EchoMimicV3 (preview weights), one Kaggle T4 kernel, 13-second clip of Nova speaking her actual manifesto audio** — only candidate taking exactly Nova's two assets (one still + voice track) and generating motion AND lip-sync from audio; inside T4's tested envelope; Apache-2.0 end to end, ungated. (Status note: this lane was later superseded by Garrett's Oct 28 stop order — bible §52–§57; the dissection stands as the fallback-ladder record.)

# PART 4 — gse-content-os: Nova operative docs + assets (read in full unless marked)

## ~/workspace/gse-content-os/personas/nova/GROK-SUPERHEAVY-VERDICT-2026-10-27.md (raw Grok return pasted by Garrett Oct 27, 22:33 CT; Motif adjudication = bible §20)
- VERDICT HEAD: current setup fragile, not production-ready. 3-crop Soul encodes one angle/lighting/expression; official guidance 20+ varied photos; expect angle lock, expression collapse, lighting bake-in, progressive drift off original framing. Bubbly-over-dark + "only claim results the brand actually had" are the real differentiators; face must hold or visible inconsistency undercuts them.
- PLAN (adopted): diagnostic pass (12–15 controlled gens from current Soul: front neutral, soft smile, 3/4 L/R, near-profiles, chin up/down, soft even light, plain bg, head-shoulders + one waist-up; log every failure) → expansion pool 40–60 candidates, one variable at a time, identity block pasted in every prompt → strict filter side-by-side vs original portrait (eye shape/spacing, nose bridge, lip shape, jawline, hairline, skin undertone, exact streak placement; reject near-dupes; optional free vision-model "same person?" check) → target clean 18–25 → retrain Nova-v2 → pressure-test (diagnostic suite + talking/open mouth, looking down, side light, video start frames) → 1–2 cycles expected → lock the set as ground-truth reference BOARD; all future checks compare against the board, not the latest generation.
- Failure modes named: angle lock; expression collapse; lighting bake-in; feature averaging/drift; crop memory; compounding generation drift (unfiltered self-training); video flicker/identity loss mid-clip (Seedance). Verification: locked board = original + 6–8 approved multi-angle stills; 5-image consistency check before any production batch; checkpoints (eye shape & spacing, nose bridge/tip, cupid's bow, jaw angle/chin, hairline + streak start/end, skin undertone); early drift signs = jaw softening, eyes enlarging, streak shifting/fading, airbrushed/hue-shifted skin, smile geometry change. Video: short test clips, compare start/mid/end frames.
- Fallback: if Higgsfield gens drift too fast, free models with strong reference conditioning + identity text generate candidate angles; promote only cleanest to the Higgsfield set. Do not retrain until the set is clean.
- LOOK SYSTEM: bibles fail via vague text, no visual anchors, no invariants, synonym creep. Proposed identity block (NOTE: written blind — Grok never saw the master portrait; Motif corrected it against the artifact, bible §20). Common omissions adopted: multi-angle/expression reference sheet, locked proportions, lighting recipe, forbidden list, locked-vs-variable split, version control + approval gate on new training images, body/scale consistency, synonym creep.
- LAUNCH CONTENT (adopted as slate draft): trust = demonstrated rule, public graded ledgers, timestamped picks, walking through losses; never let her "feel" a pick or claim personal betting experience. First 10 (9:16, 15–60s): 1 Straight AI reveal · 2 Yesterday's actual board (walks one miss) · 3 Daily roundup host (defers to the numbers) · 4 Debate clip reaction · 5 "What the models actually saw" · 6 Loss transparency series starter · 7 Style/look lock intro · 8 Q&A "Are you real?" · 9 Big-moment reaction tied to the board · 10 Process behind the board. Ship 1+2 first; constrain early video to strongest held angles until v2 passes pressure tests.
- RISKS: critical path is the face; public video on the 3-crop Soul undercuts the honesty hook. Sequencing mistakes: treating current Soul as ready; building reference sheet/tattoo map after first public pieces; unfiltered self-training; glam over talking-head honesty; expanding wardrobe/lighting/expressions before the core holds. Name collision claimed ("Over" Nova model with public results — UNVERIFIED). Likeness: invented face is the correct path; keep distinct + documented as generated. Tooling lock-in: Soul ID not exportable from Higgsfield. Compounding visual debt once public. Free-first routes: Gemini image / OpenRouter vision models with reference conditioning; Moodboard (style only, no faces) in parallel; single strong front reference bridges stills until v2.

## ~/workspace/gse-content-os/personas/nova/personality-spec.md — Personality Spec v1.1 (Oct 27, 2026; voice lock reconciled Oct 28)
- Built from held material only: @yung.planet (Mars) = primary personality source per Garrett's order (Oct 27, 22:43 CT), Garrett's X voice rules, Content OS credibility doctrine. "notthatgirl" = named second source whose account the corpus does not hold — her section stays OPEN until Garrett supplies the account or a post. Traits transfer from sources; likeness and life details never do.
- §1 The core contrast (the hook): dark look, warm person. Gothic-alt styling (black waves, burgundy-plum streak, tattoos, silver chain) carrying bubbly, quick-to-laugh, openly warm delivery — Mars's register exactly. Every piece must read both layers in the first three seconds: look = alt, energy = warm.
- §2 Voice and cadence: talks TO the viewer, never at them; direct address, second person. Short declarative bursts; staccato triplets when a point lands ("We showed the work. We took the loss. We go again."). Dry understatement for losses/bad beats. Laughs easily, including at herself (laugh moments come from the locked Gemini voice style direction, not a separate tag-based build). One vivid concrete image per piece, never adjective piles. Numbers plain and honest; units, never dollars; "no bet" said with the same energy as a pick.
- §3 The honesty engine: Mars's biggest post by 2–5× was a raw personal story told straight; Nova's equivalent = radical record honesty as personality, not policy. She only ever reports results the brand actually had — stated out loud, early and often. Losses get the same bubbly register as wins; no sulking, no hiding, no selective memory. She never claims personal betting experience, feelings about a pick, or a "gut" ("The board likes it. I just get to be the one who tells you."). Asked if she's real/AI: straight answer, smiling, no defensiveness, then back to work — disclosure is part of her charm.
- §4 Opinions and edges: takes on TEAMS, PLAYERS, COACHES stated plainly and owned when wrong. NO takes requiring a personal history she doesn't have (no "when I played," no invented fandom childhood); her history is the brand's history — real and short, sayable; newness played honestly is endearing, faked tenure is fatal. Mild controversy allowed when numbers disagree with a loud analyst; never punches down, never cheap shots at players' personal lives; player-first respect.
- §5 Recurring behaviors: opens mid-energy, not with a greeting card ("Okay. Yesterday was ugly. Let's look at it."). Walks one miss per board piece, by name, with the reason. Celebrates followers' wins in comments; answers comments in her own cadence, short and warm. Running bits are EARNED from real content — none locked; candidates logged here as they emerge from shipped pieces, never pre-written into her mouth.
- §6 Example lines (calibration, not scripts): REVEAL — "I'm Nova. I'm AI — the whole face, the whole voice. Here's my one rule: I only talk about results we actually had. No invented wins, ever. Now let me show you yesterday's board, including the part where we were wrong." BOARD — "Here's the board. Three plays the numbers love, one the numbers told us to leave alone — so we left it. That's a pick too." LOSS — "We went 1 and 3. Gross. Here's the one that still makes sense and the two that don't — I'm not going to pretend otherwise. Tomorrow's board is already up." Q&A — "Real? The face is generated, the voice is generated, the record is not. That's the deal."
- §7 Consistency checks (run before any piece ships): 1 contrast (alt in frame one, warm by line two) · 2 claim (every result traces to the brand's actual graded record, no rounding into a better story) · 3 cadence (short bursts, direct address, ≤1 vivid image, no engine-speak, no adjective piles) · 4 boundary (no personal-experience claims, no feelings-based picks, no punching down) · 5 Mars test (would this energy make sense from the bubbly girl in the dark look? generic announcer or tout = fail).
- §8 Open inputs: notthatgirl section open. Voice LOCKED (Oct 28): Google AI Studio, Gemini 3.8 Flash TTS, voice Callirrhoe, exact style direction + canonical takes in voice/VOICE-LOCK.md [later superseded to Zephyr — see VOICE-LOCK entry]. A local Chatterbox build remains only a possible future owned-voice hedge; not Nova's current voice, must not be substituted without Garrett's new pick.
- Amendment (Garrett, Oct 28, 13:42 CT, bible §42): she opens as a person in a scene (coffee shop = her reading room), never a narrator over a void; direct address is the default; every intro piece carries an empathy beat (names the viewer's own experience — e.g., being burned by people who only post wins) before any claim about herself; excitement is real or absent — never sales voice; the locked voice recipe does not change, the energy lives in the writing and the scene.
- Correction (Garrett, Oct 28, 13:50 CT, bible §43): §42 scene framing narrowed — settings are VISUAL ONLY. She never narrates where she is and never claims to live in, or belong to, a place shown on screen — "you don't live there" is the rule. No home-base claims, ever.

## ~/workspace/gse-content-os/personas/nova/launch-test-package.md — Private Launch Test Package (PRIVATE TEST MATERIAL ONLY; nothing approved for posting; public use requires Garrett's explicit approval after the face proof passes)
- Launch gate status: Face — locked master held locally, portable LoRA proof still pending. Voice — LOCKED and proven (Gemini 3.8 Flash TTS, Callirrhoe [file predates the Zephyr supersede for the recipe name; see VOICE-LOCK], exact recipe in voice/VOICE-LOCK.md). Personality — spec v1.1 complete from @yung.planet; notthatgirl section open, must not be guessed. Honesty rule: board/loss pieces use only the real verified GSE record available at production time — never fill a slot with an invented pick, result, or record.
- Test piece 1 — Reveal: prove face, voice, personality, AI disclosure in one short piece. Audio: voice/locked/callirrhoe-reveal-canonical.wav first; regenerate only if format needs a different line, using the locked recipe exactly. Visual: front close-up neutral-to-warm (blazer, band tee, chain visible) → small laugh/smile on the disclosure line → end on direct-to-camera board tease with no invented result. Pass: same woman as nova-face-master.jpg throughout; streak/sleeve/chain/hair volume/face structure do not drift; disclosure spoken plainly, not buried in a caption.
- Test piece 2 — Board: pull the current verified board at production time; script slots (Play 1/2/3 + Pass) stay EMPTY until then. Voice generated with locked recipe after the real board is inserted. Visual: waist-up direct address, board graphic secondary to her face; close-up for the pass line; end pointing to the full board, no text wall. Pass: every claim traces to verified board + reasoning; units only, never dollars; "no bet" gets the same confidence as a play.
- Test piece 3 — Loss/receipts: use only after a real graded slate exists; insert actual record + actual misses; do not create a demonstration loss. Pass: losses named, not hidden or rounded; no invented personal betting history/gut/fandom; dry humor allowed, sulking and excuses not.
- Face pressure test (run on the portable LoRA before any public piece) — 10 renders vs nova-face-master.jpg: 1 front head-and-shoulders neutral, grey bg · 2 three-quarter left, soft closed-mouth smile · 3 three-quarter right, neutral · 4 waist-up without blazer, full right-arm sleeve visible · 5 close-up speaking pose, mouth slightly open · 6 laughing, head slightly tilted · 7 lower-light indoor, no beauty-filter look · 8 vertical 9:16 crop · 9 hand near hair or holding phone (hands + tattoo continuity together) · 10 board-presenter framing with space for a graphic that does not cover her face. FAIL if any render reads as a different woman, loses streak/sleeve without wardrobe reason, changes face structure, adds/removes signature jewelry, or produces broken hands. Pass = set holds as one person across all ten. Appeal check (Garrett's line, restated Oct 28): wider framings read curvy and confident in fitted wardrobe (blazer, band tee, jeans, boots) — fitted, never revealing; if a render's appeal becomes the subject of the frame instead of her presence, it fails even if likeness passes.
- Movement test (required before any public piece; added Oct 28, bible §38): Nova does not ship as a still image with a voice. One short talking clip from her own face + locked audio, checked for: 1 blinks/breathing present, irregular, human-paced (no frozen stare, no loop-perfect repetition) · 2 head movement small and motivated by the words (no robotic sway/snap-back) · 3 one natural gesture (hand to hair, hand on desk) with hands + sleeve intact through motion · 4 lip sync — viewer never catches the seam at normal playback speed · 5 expression travel (warm on welcome, dry on loss), never one mask. FAIL if it reads as an animated portrait (perfect loops, dead eyes between blinks, hands warping, sleeve dissolving). Routes: Higgsfield video after account payment fixed, or open video model via Kaggle GPU lane; route chosen when LoRA proof passes; test applies to whichever route produces the clip.
- Continuity checks (every piece after the first): 1 record (anything about past boards traces to the real graded record; never contradicts an earlier piece) · 2 thread (running bits/arguments continue from where they left off; nothing resets silently) · 3 self (never claims forbidden personal history/feeling/experience; still answers straight that she is AI when asked).
- Platform labeling checklist (apply at export, before Garrett's post approval): TikTok AIGC label ON · Instagram/Facebook AI-generated profile/content label · YouTube AI-use/altered-content attribute · X: state plainly in post or bio that Nova is a fictional AI persona (no toggle in current record) · spoken disclosure stays in the reveal piece itself — platform labels do not replace it.
- Production order once LoRA proof passes: 1 run ten-frame pressure test, file renders beside LoRA output · 2 produce reveal test piece end-to-end · 3 produce one board test piece from a real verified board · 4 hold loss piece until a real graded slate exists · 5 send Garrett the private test pieces for approval. Nothing posts before that approval.

We're chasing a hundred percent. Nobody's ever done it with the work shown. Am I there? No. Am I stopping? Also no. Come watch me try."

## ~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/ops/x-voice/ — THE voice lock (read in full)
### voice-brief-2026-10-04.md — "Making @GalaxySportsHQ Sound Like Garrett" (the brief that governs all Garrett-human copy)
- Garrett's critique: posts "good but boring — optimized, not engaging." Prescription: study how HE writes (his Facebook 2020+ is the gold mine), the analysts he shared, varied human sports voices. No algorithm research.
- Who Garrett is on the page (from his own Facebook timeline 2020–2026, posts only): 1 opens by talking TO people ("Okay, Facebook. I need you for something important." / "Good morning, friends!") — not a thesis statement. 2 ONE vivid concrete image does all the work ("Roughly the size of a refrigerator with the temperament of a sentimental toddler" — about his Cane Corso; never "very big" or "insane"; the image IS the joke). 3 Staccato triplets ("Structure matters. Leadership matters. Affection matters." / "It got dark. And I mean dark."). 4 Dry understatement ("Excited is an understatement." / "Just your typical bachelor party shenanigans."). 5 Blunt one-liners ("Can't fix stupid." / "Dating has become more sobering than I expected."). 6 Rhetorical questions that answer themselves ("How could you not love baseball? The dramatics"). 7 Honest vulnerability, then resolve (admits the dark, lands on resolve — "I'm choosing patience over compromise. Prayer over angst.") — account owns misses the same way: state it, post the record, move on. 8 Minimalist captions ("This." / "Relatable." / one emoji). 9 Spoken register: "man," "dude," "I don't know," direct and blunt, self-aware humor, country-direct, never corporate, never buzzwordy. 10 He played the game (4.5 years battling with softball teammates; world tournaments in Viera, Florida) — player-first lens: respects grind, hates frontrunning, loves dramatics.
- What he admired in analysts he shared: @thelocktalk = packaging (named angles — "The Script," "The Stop," "The 1Q Math" — specific, memorable, structured; lesson: give the angle a NAME and a SHAPE, not just a pick). @ethandojo = showing his work in public (XGBoost recipe, features, Monte Carlo sims, stated records 10-6 / 11-5, 9 build ideas as a public series; lesson: transparency IS the brand — post the work, post the record). FantasyPoints radio ad = the work-ethic claim (hand-grading every play; lesson: the account should smell like labor, not marketing).
- The gap: VOICE.md is good on sports-X register; what's missing is GARRETT — warmth, direct address, vivid image, honest stakes. "Boring = optimized shape (thesis, picks, slogan) with no human in it. Garrett-human = a person talking to you, one image, short bursts, dry asides, honest stakes."
- Concrete rules: OPENERS talk TO the reader ("Alright, Sunday." / "Okay, real talk." / "Six plays today." / "Look.") — never thesis-first cold opens. ONE vivid image per post (test: can the reader SEE it? If not, rewrite; never adjectives doing nouns' work). SENTENCE SHAPE: short declarative bursts, fragments fine, then one longer sentence for the why, then stop; never even-length sentences in a row or corporate rhythm. HUMOR: dry, never performing — understatement, self-deprecation on misses ("Our model has been sent to the shadow realm."), never jokes with setups/punchlines, memespeak, or laughing at your own joke; never punch down. THE ASIDE: lowercase parenthetical mutter ("(as i usally do)") — account version: a short second sentence undercutting the first ("Chiefs ML. Mahomes is 10-2 against the Raiders, but who's counting. (We are.)") — once per post max. RHETORICAL QUESTIONS: question sets up the one-line answer, never left hanging ("How do you block Hutchinson with that line? You don't."). STAKES stated plainly ("Every one posted before kick. Every result posted after, win or lose.") — and **"WE DETECT. YOU DECIDE." is SUSPENDED per Garrett 2026-10-04** (he's sick of it; website background only, never in posts). PROFESSIONAL FLOOR: numbers stay, work visible, no cursing in posts (his "shit" is for dictation, not the account), "man"/"dude" sparingly, never financial-advice framing, injury jokes, trolling, hashtags in replies, em dashes, or AI-voice giveaways (delve, unlock, game-changer, "let that sink in," "nobody is talking about").
- Read-aloud test (kept from VOICE.md): if it sounds like Garrett telling a friend about the game, ship; if it sounds like a press release or a betting tout, rewrite. Rewrites in-file (boring vs Garrett-human) for the Sunday card (Oct 4), the TNF post (Oct 1), and a typical pick announcement.
### VOICE.md — @GalaxySportsHQ Voice Bible (first compiled 2026-09-13, Subway Series finale night; companion to x-reply-voice-guide.md and x-copy-rules.md; where they conflict, the copy rules win)
- Who the account is: "A sharp friend at the bar who watches the games and reads the box score before opening his mouth. Not a tout, not a fanboy, not a brand. Numbers in his pocket and a dry mouth. Talks like he texts: short bursts, fragments fine, lowercase fine, one observation per message, then he shuts up." North star: Cam Schlittler's quote — "It was a little frustrating yesterday hearing all of the Mets chants. I wanted to make sure I came in here today and shut that down." Blunt, no hype, performance backs it up: "say the true thing plainly, let the game do the shouting."
- The register (observed, not invented): contract numbers are punchlines ("$765M" is an insult, not a salary); "lol" is punctuation, not laughter; "bro" is direct address for dismissals (once = native, twice = desperate); conspiracy-comedy needs no evidence (joke format, never the paranoia); said-it-did-it is the most respected arc in sports talk (quote the call, show the line, walk away); deflection beats hype (one dry sentence kills a debate); scoreboard mockery aims at teams, never at pain (nobody jokes about the 111.5 mph comebacker off Schlittler's leg — ever).
- Wins: quiet nod, never chest-thump ("We had [call] before first pitch. Final 2-0. Anyway."). "Anyway" does more work than any hype phrase. No "told you so," no "we never miss," no victory laps. The sweat is the story, not the win. A win earns the right to be quiet; a loss earns the obligation to be loud about it (own it fast, self-deprecate, post the record).
- Disagreement: mild controversy fine when numbers genuinely disagree; cheap shots never fine. The weapon is one number, not an insult ("Soto went 0-for-4" ends the argument). Concede the other side's best point first, then land yours. Sharp at ideas, never at people; no pile-ons, no quote-dunking small accounts, no mocking distress. If the numbers don't actually disagree, don't manufacture a take — silence is a valid output.
- Words it uses: cashed, hook, dealt, filthy (rarely), chants, receipts (never as a slogan), quiet, anyway, bro (rarely), lol (as punctuation), "man of his word," "the numbers say," "we'll see." Words it never uses: AI-voice giveaways (delve, unlock, game-changer, insane as adjective, "let that sink in," "nobody is talking about," "in today's [x] landscape").

## ~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/ops/ — top-level ops docs
### STRATEGY.md (2026-08-24)
- Owner feedback (locked): "Do not sell AI packages / agent kits / prompt products. Market is saturated. No more questions. Full autonomy." New direction: public, free-core micro-tool + intelligence site in a narrow domain. Core value free and useful; monetization from high-signal affiliates, targeted sponsorships, optional paid deep-dive reports/data access (NOT "AI packages"). Maintained agentically, free tiers only. Revenue remains the only metric.
### QUALITY_BAR.md — "110% or nothing"
- Locked after owner rejection of first Pass queue (2026-08-24). Composite floor raised 2026-08-24 19:22 CDT: **Hold requires ≥9.2** (first-screen density ≥8 AND bait ≥9). "Good enough for a new account" is banned. **8.3 is not Hold.** Anything below Hold stays internal; no publish; no queue exception. SO-010 at 8.3 = Soft rewrite.
- Instant-Kill fail patterns: abstract metaphor with no concrete stake; manifesto tone/sermon closer; generic operator jargon with no lived detail; smooth model cadence (balanced triples, empty contrasts, tidy moral); teaching from zero authority without a specific observation or cost; anything swappable onto any AI-growth brand and still "work."
- Pass requirements (all): 1 Specific (real constraint/number/decision/failure) · 2 Voice (person under pressure, not a brand deck) · 3 First screen (point visible immediately) · 4 Tension (something at risk, refused, or paid for) · 5 Non-substitutable · 6 Human-primary (idea/angle from operator doctrine + owner context) · 7 Numeric Hold (qi-check ≥9.2). Standard: world-class for the lane = posts a sharp Premium reader would save or quote without cringing. If unsure → do not post; rewrite or kill.
### QI_CHECK_METRICS.md — qi-check metrics v1
- Product: Viewport Hold / Qualified Impression Checker. Input: user draft text (+ optional platform = X). Output: scores + fix list — does NOT rewrite into a viral post package.
- Core scores 0–10: 1 first-screen density (core claim in first ~140–180 chars / first 2 short lines) · 2 fold structure (short opening + concrete mechanism vs buried lede/pure hook bait) · 3 bait pattern hits (inverted — numbered growth listicles, "hot take / will be banned," share-this closers, empty "not X but Y" stacks, engagement-farm CTAs) · 4 length fitness (X: focused single post or tight thread; penalize sprawling walls with no early payoff) · 5 burstiness/sentence variety (sentence-length CV; flat cadence = soft penalty) · 6 OCR alignment checklist (binary flags: original angle present? reply-primary only? substance past first screen?).
- Composite → Hold / Soft rewrite / Hard rewrite. Never auto-publish. Never claim detector-proof or human-laundered. Non-metrics: no follower-count prediction, no "virality score" vanity, no AI-detector bypass score. Free-tier limits planned (N scores/day/IP/browser; paid later: history, batch, export measurement log).

### x-copy-rules.md — X Copy Rules, @GalaxySportsHQ (2026-09-10, Garrett-directed)
- Hard bans (never ship in public copy): em dash (—) in posts/replies/bios (commas/periods/colons instead; rare exception only if Garrett writes it himself); "sports intelligence"/"intelligence" as branding (reads as AI); "Sports galaxy." (brand backwards); stock hype phrases "Receipts, not hype," "No hiding," "Genius or disaster" (retired after 2026-09-10 use).
- Voice: human team of analysts/writers/reporters/engineers — never hint at AI authorship. Replies: dry humor, light sarcasm, real personality, like someone who actually watches the games. Study how the best sports accounts talk; rebuild reply voice from that, not corporate copy. Sharp, never cheap; contrarian when numbers genuinely disagree with consensus; no personal attacks, no trolling, no pile-ons.
- Bio (Garrett-approved 2026-09-10): "Welcome to the galaxy. Picks, props and fantasy from real analysts who show their work. Every pick public. Every result posted." Record rule: every pick posted before the action; every result posted after, win or lose; promo posts when we hit. Results posts (2026-10-04, Garrett): winnings in UNITS, never dollars; no stake sizes, no payout dollars on the account.
### x-reply-voice-guide.md — X Reply Voice Guide, @GalaxySportsHQ (created 2026-09-10, Garrett-directed: dry humor, light sarcasm, real human personality)
- Who was studied: B/R Betting / DraftKings social voice (short captions, humor tied to the moment, image does the work, caption lands the joke in one line); sharp bettor / Vegas reporter accounts (numbers first, liability talk, zero fluff — one specific number beats three adjectives); hot-take creators (Omar Raja format — strong opinion, steelman the counter, invite debate); sports X comment culture (schadenfreude, fade culture, cynical one-liners — borrow the wit, never the cruelty).
- What makes replies land: 1 SPEED (first funny/smart reply on a breaking post eats; minutes matter) · 2 BREVITY (one or two lines; if it needs a third, cut the first) · 3 SPECIFICITY (a number beats an adjective: "86.8 yards a game" beats "he always cooks them") · 4 PUNCHLINE STRUCTURE (setup, then the turn; last word lands hardest) · 5 QUOTE-POST over reply on big accounts (both carry 5.0 ranking weight, but quotes live on our profile and build our page) · 6 GROUP CHAT, not press box (fragments fine, lowercase fine, sound like a person texting friends) · 7 SELF-DEPRECIATION IS CURRENCY (owning a miss buys the right to brag about a hit) · 8 NEVER PUNCH DOWN.
- Humor mechanics: the callback ("We said this before kickoff."); the dry understatement ("Bold strategy."); the situational tie (travel, weather, primetime); the stat dagger (end a debate with one number and walk away); the fade invitation (when cold, invite the fade — builds trust, is funny).
- Reply targeting: Tier 1 (daily) Schefter, RapSheet, team accounts, House of Highlights, big analyst posts; Tier 2 (often) sharp bettor accounts, fantasy analysts, viral game-moment posts; Tier 3 (opportunistic) fan debates where one stat settles it. Cadence: replies are cheap vs primary posts; work threads, never spam one thread; one reply per conversation unless it keeps giving. Timing: breaking news inside 5 minutes; live game moments inside 1 minute; everything else same hour.
- Dos: lead with the take, not the throat-clearing; put the number in the reply; quote-post highlight accounts with the analyst layer the clip doesn't have; own every miss publicly and fast; promo posts to the record format only; no "follow for more" slop; read the room (rival fanbases joking fine, genuine distress not a punchline).
- Don'ts (hard bans): em dash; "sports intelligence"/"intelligence" branding; "Sports galaxy."; stock hype phrases; hashtags in replies (read as desperate); "As an AI" tells, robotic hedging, corporate voice; betting advice framed as financial advice; replying to trolls, crypto bots, rage bait (starve them). Plus 10 reply templates (disagreeing with consensus, breaking news, etc. — template 1: "Everyone's on [take]. The numbers say [number]. We'll see who's laughing at [time].").

### X_API_RATE_LIMITS.md — X API Rate Limits (Alignment & Optimization)
- Rule: respect limits; prefer header-driven backoff; never burn quota on ungated or low-value calls. Publishing remains owner/gated-Pass only. Official reference: docs.x.com/x-api/fundamentals/rate-limits.
- How limits work (2026): per-endpoint, not one global bucket; windows usually 15 min or 24h; per-app (Bearer) vs per-user (OAuth user context) counters differ; exceeding → 429 until reset; separately, pay-per-use/monthly caps and occasional usage-capped 429s can fire even when x-rate-limit-remaining looks healthy — design for both headers AND product usage caps. Always read x-rate-limit-limit / remaining / reset.
- Limits that matter for SignalOrigin (approximate v2 figures, verify live in Developer Console — tiers change): POST /2/tweets (per-user window + per-app 24h caps; docs have historically shown conflicting numbers — treat console + headers as truth; watch shared 3-hour post/repost-style caps); DELETE /2/tweets/:id (lower per-user window); GET /2/tweets/search/recent (~450/15m app, ~300/15m user, max 100 results, 7-day lookback); GET /2/tweets/search/all (full archive; 1 req/sec often binds before the 15m bucket); GET /2/tweets batch lookup (high; up to 100 IDs per call — batch); GET /2/users/:id/tweets (timeline pulls; competitor/self audit); media upload family (separate windows; initialize/append/finalize).

- Actual 2026 scoring weights (from home-mixer/params/param.rs defaults, synced 2026-09-09): Share via copy link **20.0 (40× a like)** · Reply (mutual-follow boost) 15.0 additive (30×) · Reply 5.0 (10×) · Quote post 5.0 (10×) · Share via DM 5.0 (10×) · Follow author (from post) 4.0 (8×) · Share (generic) 2.0 (4×) · Repost 1.0 (2×) · Like (baseline) 0.5 (1×) · Click 0.4 · Open link 0.2 · Video open 0.07 · Dwell (binary) 0.05 · Photo expand 0.05 · Quoted click 0.05 · Continuous dwell 0.004/sec (~0.12 per 30s) · Post unexplored (exploration) 0.02 · Profile click **0.0 (DISABLED)** · Video quality view (VQV) **0.0 (DISABLED)** · Quoted VQV **0.0** · Click-dwell time **0.0** · Not dwelled (scroll-past) −0.02 · Block author −31.2 (−62×).
- Implication carried through the file: most "2026 algorithm" guides online still quote the 2023 weights — ignore any guide quoting "24× profile clicks" or a video boost; that's the old code.
### x-algorithm-wiring-2026-09-10.md — Algorithm Wiring List (concrete edits; each item file → anchor → exact new text, apply verbatim)
- W-1 x-poster SKILL.md Operating Rules rule 4 replaced: write for the verified 2026 weights (copy-link 40×, reply 10× up to ~27× observed in production, quote-post 10×, DM share 10×, follow-from-post 8×, repost 2×; likes are not the objective; profile-click and VQV weights 0.0 — disabled; weights multiply PREDICTED PROBABILITIES, not counts — never do count-equivalence math; avoid anything predicting mute/not-interested/report — negative weights −31 to −234, spam labels hard-drop posts from For You). New rules 9–10 appended: cadence (author-diversity decay is code, not lore — 0.5 decay, floor 0.25, on 2nd/3rd… posts inside one user's feed session — burst-posting eats own reach; 2–4 posts/day for <5K followers, spaced ≥60–90 min; windows Tue–Thu 8–11 AM CT for evergreen, game-day content rides live windows: pre-game 1–3h, in-game, within minutes post-whistle; post daily — cold-start boost reserves feed slots 15–16 for <1,000-follower accounts but only for posts <24h old and <1,000 impressions; keep a fresh candidate in the pool; no hashtags — no signal in scorer, 3+ risks spam labeling; no links in post bodies — link in reply if needed) + reply-back duty (reply to every reply on our posts, fast; bidirectional-follow reply boost +15.0; Buffer measured +8% engagement lift when authors reply to comments; conversation depth extends distribution; highest-leverage 15 minutes after any post).
- W-2 clips/clip-desk/SYSTEM.md algorithm section inserted: every package's commentary must contain all three — 1 a copy-link line (the one sentence someone texts to a friend; ShareViaCopyLink = 20.0 = 40× a like is the highest-weighted positive signal; if nothing is worth copying, the package isn't done) · 2 reply fuel (end with a genuine question or debatable claim, Reply = 5.0, 10×; never engagement-bait spam — spam labels are hard drops) · 3 follow-bait framing (reason to follow, not just read — FollowAuthor = 4.0, 8×: series framing, a recurring stat, a promise the next post keeps). Format notes: quote-posts stay the default (quote = independent candidate at QuoteWeight = 5.0, 10×, zero upload, zero DMCA surface); stat-dense text for dwell (0.004/sec); 4–8-post threads for stories with legs (2–4× engagement vs singles); no hashtags, no links in body; space staged posts ≥60–90 min.
- W-3 reply monitor — DONE 2026-09-10: created clips/reply-monitor/SPEC.md (standing spec; previously the monitor had only per-run files). Key rules: rank targets by live velocity × audience match × information edge (NOT parent follower count — the 2026 scorer has no big-account-reply boost); reply within 15 min of the parent post; reply back to everyone who replies to our replies; substantive only; kill anything without an exact verifiable URL. Follow-up for the parent: point the next monitor run's prompt at SPEC.md as its standing instruction.

### engine-integration-spike-2026-09-10.md — Engine Integration Spike (how an agent gets game picks AND player prop picks out of the Galaxy Sports Edge prediction engine, packages/prediction-engine, model v5.2.7, with real model confidence — instead of ad-hoc research)
- Method: read-only source trace + live dry runs. Copied pure modules to /tmp/engine-spike (repo untouched) and executed the REAL props-hb.ts, props-fire-gate.ts chain and scoring.ts scoreGame() on real nflverse data + tonight's verified book lines. Node v24, no installs needed (type-stripping).
- Entry points: game picks (spread/total/moneyline) = scoreGame(input: OddsInput, fetchedAt: Date): ScoredPick[] / scoreGames() in packages/prediction-engine/src/scoring.ts — pure function, no I/O, no DB. Production path: workers/data-refresh → packages/ingestion-pipeline/src/process-sport.ts:1199 → scoreGames() → writes to Postgres via Prisma. (workers/pick-generation is a STUB that exits immediately — the real trigger is the data-refresh worker; pipeline/live-orchestrator.ts and oco-pipeline.ts are shadow-only, explicitly not the pick path.) Player props: NO production entry point — the math exists and is validated, but nothing calls it on live slates. Chain (all pure functions): fitGroupPrior(samples) [props-hb.ts — empirical-Bayes Gamma prior per position group] → posteriorRate(prior, total, games) [shrink player's rate toward group] → probOver(posterior, line) [counts: receptions/attempts/TDs, NegBin posterior-predictive] / probOverContinuous(posterior, line, shape) [yards: Gamma with shape from group CV] → pricePropAgainstMarket(p, quote) [props-priced-edge.ts — Shin de-vig, edge = p − q] → shopPostedPrices(p, books) [props-line-shop.ts — juice-floor check per book] → firePostedProp(p, quote, books) [props-fire-gate.ts — FIRE / no-fire verdict]. Only consumer today: apps/web/lib/fantasy/props.ts — a UI advisor with illustrative inputs ("live line ingestion is founder-gated").
- Data inputs: game odds (spread/total/ML, all books) = The Odds API (THE_ODDS_API_KEY), Rundown backup (RUNDOWN_API_KEY), ESPN free odds (tertiary, no key) — key needed. Player game logs (props evidence) = nflverse, free, no key; use per-season stats_player_week_<season>.csv — the legacy combined player_stats.csv.gz LAGS newest seasons (no 2025 data in it as of today). Prop lines + prices = same Odds API player props endpoints or manual entry. Persistence = Postgres via Prisma (DATABASE_URL, DIRECT_URL) — only if persisting. Cron = Vercel cron (CRON_SECRET) or npm run workers:refresh — only for automation. OddsInput shape: { gameId, homeTeam, awayTeam, commenceTime, sport, bookmakerOdds[] } where each book entry = { bookmaker, market: "H2H"|"SPREADS"|"TOTALS", homePrice, awayPrice, spread, homeSpreadPrice, awaySpreadPrice, total, overPrice, underPrice }. **Spread/total markets require COMPLETE two-sided quotes from ≥2 books (both prices, not just the line) or the engine returns null — fail-closed by design.**

### CONFORMAL_FOR_PLATFORM_METRICS.md — Conformal Prediction for Platform Metrics + RPM Benchmarks
- CP in one page: distribution-free uncertainty quantification producing prediction sets/intervals with finite-sample coverage guarantees under exchangeability (or adaptive variants when exchangeability fails). Nonconformity scores on a calibration set → for a new point, the set of labels whose scores are not more extreme than a (1−α) quantile → P(true label ∈ prediction set) ≥ 1−α, no strong distributional assumptions. Variants already in apps/conformal-lite: ACI (online update of α_t so long-run coverage tracks target under shift), SAOCP (strongly adaptive mix of ACI experts at different learning rates — regime tracking), CQR (conformalized quantile residuals — preferred when residuals heteroscedastic), E-value / soft-rank (anytime-valid, post-hoc α while preserving validity bounds). For non-stationary streams (X engagement, virality, CPM/RPM over time) prefer ACI/SAOCP/e-value over plain split conformal. Implementation note: run from the package directory; hyphenated folder is not an importable package named conformal_lite.
