### OCR_ALIGNMENT.md — X Original Content Rewards, alignment & optimization (active doctrine; source: X Help — Original Content Rewards, as of 2026-08)
- Rule: maximize LEGITIMATE eligibility and qualified-impression yield. No policy evasion, no fake engagement, no aggregator games.
- What the program pays for: qualified impressions = unique impressions from Premium users on the Home Timeline where ≥50% of the post is visible. NOT: reply impressions, pure quote-farming, non-Premium views, off-timeline surfaces as primary meter.
- Eligibility (must clear ALL): active Premium / Premium+ / Premium Business; ≥500 verified followers; ≥500,000 Home Timeline impressions from verified users in last 90 days (replies excluded); 18+, eligible country, good standing, personal or business account (not political/gov org); actively post original content as defined by X. Admission is not automatic even if thresholds are met — application required.
### STYLOMETRY_FEATURES.md — Stylometry Feature Engineering (feature inventory for the originality/voice gate; human-primary rule unchanged — features score production, they do not launder AI)
- Feature families to engineer: Lexical (type–token ratio, MATTR, MTLD, HD-D, VocD-D, MSTTR; hapax legomena rate, Yule's K/I; average word length, long-word ratio; function-word frequencies — core of Burrows' Delta; content-word vs function-word rates; character n-grams esp. 2–6 and word n-grams 1–2 as sparse vectors) · Syntactic (POS ratios — pronouns, conjunctions, prepositions, modals; sentence length mean + variance — feeds burstiness; dependency depth/distance, passive rate, clausal density; T-unit/clause length; mixed syntactic n-grams where parse cost is acceptable) · further families in file (semantic, discourse, author-specific).
### X402_CDP_INTEGRATION.md — x402 + CDP wallets integration plan (for Signal Origin / qi-check micropayments; CDP = Coinbase Developer Platform, not "CPD")
- x402 in one page: HTTP-native payment standard (Linux Foundation x402 Foundation, 2026) reviving HTTP 402 Payment Required. Flow: 1 client requests resource · 2 server returns 402 + payment requirements (price, asset, network, payee) · 3 client retries with signed payment (stablecoin, often USDC) in payment headers · 4 facilitator verifies + settles on-chain; server returns the resource. Roles: client (human or agent) · resource server (us) · facilitator (e.g., CDP-hosted; free tier ~1k tx/mo then small fee). Why it matters: pay-per-score / pay-per-API without accounts, API keys, or Stripe customer objects; agents can pay; humans with wallets can pay. V2 adds multi-chain, discovery, wallet identity (less repay-every-call friction). Ecosystem: spec + SDKs (TS/Python/Go), middleware one-liners, Stripe/Visa/MC among foundation members (fiat paths evolving); volume real (tens of millions of txs, mostly sub-dollar).
### X_RANKING_AND_SO010.md — X ranking factors (2026) + SO-010 engagement analysis
- Sources: public xai-org/x-algorithm home-mixer defaults (param.rs, ~Aug 2026 sync) + official OCR help language. Weights scale P(action), not raw counts. Ranking formula shape: Final Score ≈ Σ (weight_i × P(action_i | viewer, post)). Candidates from in-network (Thunder) and out-of-network (Phoenix). Out-of-network and replies/reposts get discounts vs strong originals in many paths. New-author boost can lift low-impression authors toward a target position.
- Positive coefficients (published defaults): Share via copy link 20.0 · Reply on original from mutual follow 20.0 (5 + 15 boost) · Reply 5.0 · Quote 5.0 · Share via DM 5.0 · Follow author 4.0 · Share 2.0 (further coefficients + the SO-010 analysis in file).

### LINK_TRIAGE.md — Link triage + mutual-reply boost + OCR thresholds (2026-08-24; owner dump triaged — most links are noise for Signal Origin)
- Mutual reply boost mechanics (from xai-org/x-algorithm param.rs + BIDIRECTIONAL_BOOST_CHANGE docs): base ReplyWeight = 5.0; BidirectionalFollowReplyWeightBoost = 15.0 (production after July 2026 A/B; tested 5/10/15/20); effective reply coefficient on an original post from an author you mutually follow ≈ 20.0. Boost applies to the PREDICTED reply probability on THEIR original posts in your feed — NOT "your reply floats because you replied to a mutual." BidirectionalFollowDwellWeightBoost = 0.0 (not shipped). Replies/reposts themselves are weaker candidates for out-of-network; originals index better.
- For @SignaL_OriginHQ (0 followers): mutual boost does NOTHING until real bidirectional follows exist. Path: earn mutuals in a niche by being worth following — not mass-follow games. Early reach = new-author effects + out-of-network retrieval if the post predicts high-value actions for some viewers. Do not build strategy around mutual boost until graph exists.

### SYNTHESIS_20K.md — 20,000-ft synthesis (2026-08-24 evening)
- Facilitator architecture (x402): facilitator = optional but recommended service that VERIFIES signed payment payloads and SETTLES them on-chain so the resource server never talks to a chain. CDP Facilitator APIs: POST /v2/x402/verify · POST /v2/x402/settle. Auth: CDP API key. Screening: OFAC/KYT. Networks: Base, Polygon, Arbitrum, World, Solana (ERC-20 via EIP-3009 or Permit2; SPL on Solana).
- Seller flow with facilitator: 1 client request · 2 server 402 + requirements · 3 client pays, retries with signature · 4 server → facilitator verify · 5 if valid → server does work (e.g., qi-check) · 6 server → facilitator settle · 7 200 + resource + payment response header. We never run a node; never validate signatures ourselves unless we choose to.

Original standalone posts index better for non-follower For You than reply-primary. Negative: report/mute/not interested are catastrophic; bait and generic AI cadence raise that risk. OCR path still cares about Premium HT + originality; ranking weights ≠ payout rules but both hate sludge.

### SO002_REWRITE.md — SO-002 v2, "Qualified impressions are the only impressions" (candidate only; NOT published; authorship: human-primary angle — OCR qualified-impression definition + pre-account proxy discipline; AI production only; 2026-08-25)
- Candidate body: most dashboards count every glance the same; OCR does not. A qualified impression is a unique Premium user on the Home Timeline with ≥50% of the post visible; replies do not count; non-Premium does not count; below the fold does not count. Until the account meter is live we run a proxy template on every draft before originality scoring (proxy fields table: first-screen claim → core refusal/mechanism in first 140–180 chars; viewport hold → would a time-poor Premium operator stop past the open line?; further fields in file).
### SO005_REWRITE.md — SO-005 v2, "Burrows' Delta is a better voice check than vibes" (candidate only; NOT published; authorship: human-primary angle — voice consistency as gate, not aesthetic; AI production only; 2026-08-25)
- Candidate body: we stopped arguing about whether a draft "sounds like us" — we measure it. Burrows' Delta compares relative frequencies of high-frequency function words in a candidate against our Pass-only reference corpus; lower Δ = closer stylistic match; high Δ + flat sentence-length variation = automation-risk flag before adversarial review starts. Internal pair in file: same core idea, different production quality (generic "before" Δ high vs corpus; Pass-style "after" Δ low vs sample-a/b).

### lead-magnet-prop-playbook.md — The Prop Edge Playbook ("How soft lines get found before the books move"; by the analysts at Galaxy Sports Edge; "We show our work.")
- The uncomfortable truth: sportsbooks love props because the average bettor is terrible at them — most bet props on vibes ("He's due." / "Prime time player, prime time game." / "My gut says over."). Books price that in; every "he's due" ticket is margin. But props are also the softest market in sports betting: fewer eyeballs, slower line movement, books that copy each other's numbers instead of pricing every player from scratch. If you know where to look, the edge is sitting in broad daylight. This playbook is how we look.
- Rule 1: Bet the number, not the player. (Further rules in file — prop-edge method, line-shopping discipline, book-copying tells.)

center ≈$500) → **LOWER → $550** (25% over; $550 OBO, accept $475+, walk below $450; ⚠ assumes BASE auto — if numbered refractor (Orange Wave /25 guides ~$995), $625 would be UNDER market; verify the card back first). 3 2021 Pokémon Chilling Reign Galarian Slowking V #179 PSA 10 — $350, sold $300–$325 (5 sales in 10 days) → **LOWER → $325** (14% over; $325 OBO, accept $300+, floor $300). 4 Tyreek Hill Prizm Gold Vinyl /5 PSA 9, Fireworks 2020 — $275, no exact completed sales in 60d (/5 pop has no price history) → **KEEP $275 — LOW CONFIDENCE** (next buyer's offer sets a /5 market; entertain $225+, counter $250). 5 2025 Pokémon Taruka Magikarp Japanese PSA 10 — $128, no sold comps (promo deck card, no pricecharting entry; sibling Taruka PSA 10s retail $74.99) → **LOWER → $85 — LOW CONFIDENCE**. 6 2023 Bowman Chrome U #16 Shedeur Sanders Pink Refractor PSA 10 (1st) — $120, PSA 10 $49.99 (6/1) → **LOWER → $55** (140% over — worst mispricing in the audit; accept to $45). 7 1968 Topps Floyd Little Rookie #173 SGC 6 — $100, SGC 6 $43 (6/7) → **LOWER → $50** (80–130% over; accept to $40). 8 Elly De La Cruz 2024 Topps Chrome 1989 35th Anniversary #89CB-11 PSA 9 — $40, PSA 9 sales $14.99–$32.50 (Mar–Apr; no sales in 60d, thin market) → **LOWER → $25** (median ~$20; accept to $20). 9 2022 Pokémon Astral Radiance Hisuian Sneasler V #174 PSA 10 — $40, market $51.00 → **RAISE → $48** (22% BELOW market — leaving money; list $48–50, accept to $42). 10 Needoh Gummy Bear (NIB) — $10, retail $6.99 → **LOWER → $7** (43% over manufacturer retail; accept $5). 11 Needoh Peace O' Cake (NIB) — $10, specialty retail $10.25, eBay new $11.87 (13 sold) → **KEEP $10** (just under retail and under eBay new comps; hold firm, accept $8). 12 Needoh Nice Cream Cone (NIB) — $10, eBay new $24.98 BIN, $24.99 (69 sold), $14.95 (31 sold) → **RAISE → $15** (viral TikTok variant; ask $15, accept $12). 13 Needoh Nice-sicle (NIB) — $10, retail $7.99 (Nordstrom), Poshmark new-sealed $42 → **RAISE → $12** (resale $19–42 vs $10 ask; $10 floor).

## ~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/ops/x-engagement/ (worker prompt + 4 log files)
### WORKER-PROMPT.md — X Engagement Worker, self-contained run prompt (background subagent for @GalaxySportsHQ; Garrett's standing order: "Find engagement — find future customers — engage with them. Learn from how people talk and gain a personality.")
- Hard constraints: worker cannot operate a live browser — everything produced is STAGED for a browser-task run to post from the logged-in @GalaxySportsHQ session; never claim you posted anything; say "staged." Max 4 replies per run — one strong reply beats four okay ones. NEVER: post original picks, claim pick math/win rates/calibration numbers, DM strangers, attack anyone, pitch in sports threads, mock genuine distress, quote-dunk small accounts, joke about player injuries. Human-primary: nothing staged gets posted without the parent agent's explicit go; staging is not approval.
- Voice: read and obey ops/x-voice/VOICE.md fully before writing anything. Five-line core: 1 sharp friend at the bar who reads the box score before talking (dry, plain, lets the game shout) · 2 contract numbers and box scores are the weapons (one stat ends arguments; insults never start them) · 3 wins get a quiet nod ("Anyway."), never a victory lap; losses owned fast and loudly · 4 mild controversy fine when numbers genuinely disagree; concede the other side's best point, then land yours with the number · 5 never AI-voice, never hashtags in replies, never em dashes, never press-release cadence. Run the 3-question voice check on every reply before staging (swap test, specific-number test, read-it-out-loud test).
- The run: 1 voice research first — use browser.search to read REAL current sports conversation on X tonight (postgame threads, betting Twitter, fan debates); study the register; add a dated addendum to VOICE.md only when you observe something NEW from real conversation (never update from theory) · 2 find 3–5 live threads with real traction (night's games, active debates where one stat settles it, injury-scare threads — empathy only, betting-Twitter reaction); verify each target is actually live via search (further steps in file).

### data/content-os/creators/NOVA_SOUL.md — "single source of truth" for Nova (`@GSE_QuantHost`) on main
- Identity: Nova = `@GSE_QuantHost`, "Galaxy Sports Edge's flagship on-camera Quant Desk Presenter and Roundup Host". Merges a Novus SOUL.md identity, a Higgsfield Soul ID, and the GSE Content OS 14-section creator schema (W-09).
- Locked visual block (copy-paste verbatim into every Soul 2.0 / Kling 3.0 / Veo 3.1 prompt): mid-20s athletic woman, long dark brunette/black wavy hair with faint cyan sheen, extensive blackwork + geometric mathematical sleeve tattoos on forearms, small silver septum + ear piercings, modest fitted matte-black high-collar leather/techwear zip jacket over charcoal ribbed crew-neck with thin #00E5FF orbital-cyan piping; zero cleavage/clubwear/hats/sunglasses; obsidian quant studio #05070B with Orbital Cyan #00E5FF + Ultraviolet #7B61FF volumetric data glass. Visual doctrine: 80% holographic data / 20% midground conductor.
- Reference trio for delivery/pacing: @itslilypod (calm authority), @yung.planet (visual telemetry cadence), @godnotthisgirlagain (dry deadpan receipt grading).
- Voice route as written in this file: Studio/cloud = Gemini `Callirrhoe` or `en-US-AvaNeural` (+5% rate, 55Hz sub-bass telemetry bed at −22 dB); local permissive route = Chatterbox TTS (Apache-2.0).
- Two-mode doctrine: Mode A Public Zero-Leakage Promo (frontier telemetry, free PASS/trap receipts, "Grade Me" settled-record ritual, active plays shown as `[LOCKED // 61/100]` → galaxysportsedge.com/packages) vs Mode B VIP Member / Post-Lock Reveal (full unlocked card inside /packages, public only after kickoff/settlement).
- Structure template (30–60s): 0–3s hook (one verified counter-intuitive number) → 3–18s mechanism/X-ray (3 staccato proof beats: Usage → Trench/Health → Concept/Coverage) → 18–26s free PASS receipt + one honest risk boundary → 26–35s receipts + paywall CTA.
- Voice rules: staccato triplets + one clean declarative verdict; signature lines ("Look at the number the box score hides…", "Here is your free pass receipt for tonight…"); banned: "Lock of the century", "Free money", "Whale play", "AI predicts", confidence formatted as XX%; no emojis on telemetry boards. Confidence is always a score /100.
- Hard rules: never leak an active publishAllowed pick pre-kickoff; stakes in units, never dollars (package prices $15/$39/$49 are the only permitted dollar figures, CTA slot only); never hide a loss — Monday "Grade Me" opens with the week's worst miss.
- Packet-fields whitelist + public-mode filter: when surface != VIP_VAULT and game is PRE_KICKOFF, selections with decision PLAY are auto-masked as `[LOCKED IN PACK]`.
- Cadence: game days = 1 public Trench X-Ray reel (9:16, IG/TikTok/X/Shorts) + 1 four-slide public carousel + 1 VIP vault reveal video; Mon & Fri = "Grade Me" receipts audits.
- KPIs: primary = /packages checkout CTR + 3-second hook hold ≥65%; secondary = saves-to-views ≥4.0% on carousels.
- Living sections: §9 "What I Got Wrong" (Week 7 OL-continuity lesson → Stage 1 weights now require Friday practice-participation confirmation), §12 running threads (Rush Concept Paradox, Public Steam Graveyard, Pass Discipline Counter — 9 of 13 refused in Week 8), §13 lessons (2026-10-29: free PASS receipts build 3× the authority of free picks while protecting 100% of VIP value).

## 4. Repo publishing system & doctrine docs

### docs/content-surfaces.md — "The Four Recurring Publishing Rhythms" (Phase 5, Human Marketing)
- Four surfaces, all under /observatory + email (+ social where noted): (1) "What I'm watching tonight" — daily, ~3h before slate publish, 150–300 words; (2) "Why the model stayed quiet" — as-needed per held/no-pick game, 100–200 words, mandatory closing line "I'm telling you this so the silence is legible, not so you bet it."; (3) "Signal autopsy" — weekly Monday, 400–700 words, one settled pick win or loss; (4) "One number that moved" — 2–3×/week, 60–120 words, hero-typography number, fixed closer "That's the number. Make of it what you will."
- Editorial rules for all four: first-person singular ("I built this", never "we"); no unbacked prediction language; every number cites a source; no promotional CTAs in body; no emojis; banned-phrase linter before publish; read-aloud test; voice authority = docs/brand/brand-guidelines.md; brand-safety = docs/brand-safety-rules-v2.md (no fabricated stats, no Kelly/true-EV until activated).
- Content-as-code flow: markdown in docs/content-archive/{surface}/ with a frontmatter schema (surface, publishedAt, relatedPickIds, relatedFactorKeys, gameId/settledPickId, status), build-time linter validates factor keys exist, picks exist, "quiet" items reference non-published picks, "autopsy" items reference picks settled ≥24h, no banned phrases. First-30-days target corpus: ~50–70 pieces. Explicitly NOT a blog, NOT picks marketing, NOT volume growth — "the thinking is the product".

### docs/content-automation.md
- Auto blog pipeline: picks.generated event → ContentWorker groups picks by sport/date → Claude API writes from structured pick data only → BlogPost stored → free preview (first 2 paragraphs) + premium full article.
- Non-negotiables: no fabricated stats (Claude receives only real picks data), every claim traceable to ingestion data, no guaranteed-outcome language, gambling disclaimer on all posts; Claude is never asked to recommend picks, only to write analysis around completed picks. SEO: title "{Sport} Picks {Date}: {Top Pick} Analysis", slug {sport}-picks-{date}, JSON-LD Article schema.

### docs/media/CONTENT_PILLAR_MAP.md (updated 2026-07-04)
- 10 pillars, each with purpose/audience/formats/hooks/repo tie-in/revenue tie-in/compliance notes/cadence/CTA/failure mode: Market Mirage · No-Bet Clinic · Loss Autopsy · Player Signal Lab · GSE Lab / Building the Engine · Sports Data / API Business · Founder Build Log · Betting Psychology / Decision Discipline · Partner / Tool Reviews · Weekly GSE Board Meeting.
- Pillar rules: every pillar maps to a repo evidence surface or a draft-only plan; sponsor/affiliate disclosure always; betting posts stay analytical/educational; no copyrighted broadcast footage assumed usable; no founder story replaces shipped proof; no GSN network claim may imply a mature media network before it exists.

### docs/media/CONTENT_COMPLIANCE_POLICY.md (updated 2026-07-04)
- Blocked claim patterns: "lock", "guaranteed", "risk free", "free money", "can't lose", "100% winner", "sure thing", "easy money", "mortgage play", "max bet", "all in", "hammer this", "printing money", "can't miss"; no copyrighted-footage assumption.
- Evidence-required before public use (evidence + sample window + source + approval): win rate, ROI, profit, verified, proven, calibrated, beats market, CLV, +EV; "calibrated probability" needs calibration-threshold evidence + public-claim approval.
- Sponsor/affiliate: disclosure near the mention, YouTube paid-promotion controls, sponsor copy separated from editorial, sponsors cannot control picks/outputs/no-bet decisions/autopsies/claims. Sportsbook/DFS offers need terms URL, eligible/restricted states, responsible-gaming text. No automated betting, no personalized financial advice. AI-generated imagery/narration requires disclosure + operator review.
- Review workflow (7 steps, no auto-publish path approved): draft → claim scan → source/evidence review → disclosure review → rights/visual review → operator approval → manual publish by a human.

### docs/media/content-provenance-and-review.md — "Doctrine. Binding on all published content."
- Provenance chain: published claim → Evidence Vault item → source tier T1–T6 → Source Acquisition Mesh entry → original primary source. A claim is PROVENANCE-INVALID if it has no vault item, rests on a T5/T6 primary source, has an expired evidence TTL, or its source is RED in the Source Risk Register. Every published piece needs a provenance record (fields specified: claims with evidence source/tier/freshness/governance check, AI tools used, rights, review log).
- Six review tiers by content type: T1 pick cards/Brain answers (scanner + evidence completeness + freshness + paywall-gate check + operator approval; owner approval if a win-rate claim is included) · T2 Model Journal (win-rate claims need ≥30 settled picks, defined window, model version) · T3 Loss Room autopsies (CALLED/DID_NOT_HAPPEN cross-reference) · T4 Almanac essays · T5 social posts (lightweight record) · T6 video (script + visual claim review, rights check, full final-cut review; owner approval for investor demos, win-rate claims, new categories).
- Failure handling: pre-publish failure blocks and is documented; post-publish failure → remove/correct immediately, affected pick voided in the Signal Ledger, severity P0 (false win-rate claim) / P1 (false intelligence claim) / P2 (attribution/disclosure). AI provenance: tool, output, and operator changes must be logged — "Claude wrote this" is never a sufficient record. Six Codex audit requirements close the doc (incl. no pick publishes without a logged operator APPROVED event).

### .claude/agents/content-publishing-agent.md
- Repo-internal agent spec for blog/journal content, SEO, publishing pipeline, compliance review of generated copy. DRAFT-ONLY by design: "Do NOT use it to make content go live — publishing is a manual, human action."
- Scope: apps/web/lib/content (workflow.ts), lib/content-engine (build-draft, compliance, honest-record, persist-draft, readiness, source-coverage, templates), lib/content-generator.ts, lib/studio, lib/bot-outbox, lib/journal, lib/compliance-scanner, workers/content-publishing.
- Rules that bite: CLAUDE.md rule 2 — every numeric claim must pass claude-api/numeric-guard.ts against a real GroundingSet; rule 8 — brand positioning "We're not AI. We're math you can read", every string passes compliance-scanner Layer-1 platform bans (+Layers 2–4), canonical doctrine docs/positioning.md, enforced by scripts/guardrails/trust-gate.mjs + `npm run lint:brand`. (File notes CLAUDE.md's cited positioning-vocab.json does not exist on disk.)
- Hard stop: scripts/guardrails/draft-only.mjs fails CI on any publish-side write (publishedAt writes, status→"PUBLISHED" Prisma payloads, engine-triggerable social/email/SMS/webhook sends) anywhere in apps/web, workers, packages; one whitelisted exception (watchlist email-channel.ts, founder-approved, not this agent's domain).

## 5. ~/workspace/custom-skills/local-x-poster/SKILL.md — the X posting pipeline (read in full)

Pipeline: voice-locked draft → qi-check gate → approve-desk (Garrett) → post (browser or X API) → verify + ACTION_LOG. Folder also holds assets/draft-template.md, references/gate-checklist.md, references/posting-paths.md.

- Drafting: voice lock = dry/sarcastic humor + empathy + high intelligence, no fluff; angle from owner doctrine or real owner context, never generic AI-growth-account wisdom; one point in the first screen, ~160 characters carries the post; no hashtags, engagement bait, or CTA unless deliberate; GSE pick math, calibration numbers, and win rates stay OUT of engine copy (site links allowed as CTAs); drafts saved to ~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/ops/x-drafts/<id>.md, ID format XP-YYYYMMDD-NN.
- Scorer: qi-check lives at ~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/apps/archive/qi-check (find it if the path moved; a scorer that cannot be located is a named blocker — never claim the gate ran without scorer output). Run the real scorer (scoreDraft from lib/score.js, platform 'x'), no eyeballing; record score + exact scorer path in the draft file.
- Gate thresholds: Hold (staging-eligible) = composite ≥9.2 AND first-screen density ≥8 AND bait ≥9, plus adversarial checks in references/gate-checklist.md. Soft-rewrite score → rewrite once against the fix list, re-score; still short → kill or escalate to Garrett. Hard-rewrite score → kill outright. Anything below Hold stays internal: no publishing, no queueing.
- Approval: stage the approve-desk block (references/posting-paths.md) with the score summary; nothing moves until Garrett approves in the desk or replies "APPROVE XP-YYYYMMDD-NN" — silence is not approval.
- Direct-order exception: when Garrett names exact text and orders it posted, that is approval for that exact text only; clean only what he pasted on a clean-and-post order; post even if the gate hasn't cleared/run, but never describe it as gate-passed; run the scorer after the fact and log the actual score/band + the direct-order fact.
- Posting/logging: paste the exact draft text, no edits at post time; verify the post rendered at its URL with matching text; record URL + timestamp in the draft, status → posted; append draft ID, score, slot, post URL to ~/workspace/vendor/autonomous-revenue-engine/ops/ACTION_LOG.md under today's date; engagement check at 24–48h weights replies/quotes over likes. Post failure → log, retry once, escalate; never blind-retry (a duplicate is worse than no post). Every run ends in one stated state: posted with URL / staged awaiting Garrett / killed with reason / rewrite requested.
- Closing doctrine: post nothing when nothing clears Hold and no direct order covers the text — silence is a valid output; one strong post beats cadence. Secrets/API keys never in drafts, desk blocks, logs, or the repo.

HeyGen (presenter/avatar video), Runway, Krea, FLUX, vidIQ, Upload-Post, Canva.
- **social-content-performance/SKILL.md** — Social Content Performance: analyze only accounts the user manages, taken from the connector's own account list (never substitute); use connector metric definitions, keep metrics at the level returned (account totals ≠ post performance; missing = unknown, not zero); Instagram owned-post insights are lifetime, not period — compare similar-age posts; separate reach/impressions/views/engagement/conversions and organic vs paid; no causes inferred from correlations; analysis alone never authorizes publishing or campaign changes.

- Three structural causes named: (1) **wrong feed surface** — the sweeps read the browser profile's For You feed, which serves math/AI/tool content heavily; Garrett's phone feed, trained by his actual engagement, is NFL-analytics-dense — same account, different inventory; (2) **feed-skim depth** — sweeps took at most 1 post per account from the surface while Garrett takes 4–5 from elite accounts (@sfdata9ers alone published ≥5 engine-grade tables in ~8 hours; all lanes combined caught at most 1–2); (3) **recurring series are invisible to skims** — a watchlist pull catches every edition, a skim catches one only if the algorithm serves it that hour.
- The fix applied that night: the X sweep gained an **account-deep watchlist pass** (open each watchlist profile, dissect its newest posts), watchlist seeded from the video + the verified elite set: @sfdata9ers, @PattonAnalytics, @boxscorescout, @ghatz_28, @HaydenWinks, @nfl_simulator, @ProGridSports, @KoalatyStats, @SumerSports, @run_the_sims, @The_Coach_A. The 12 video items became the first catch-up targets (all <24h old, still on the profiles). The nfl_simulator repo was slated for a full repo dossier — a Deserve-to-Win/luck layer is a direct candidate input for GSE postgame grading and calibration.
- The analysis's own gap audit: @ProGridSports term grep not checked (item 12); the Oct 29 AM analytics sweep's 91 CSVs live in the remote Sports repo, not this checkout (its delivered scorecard credits it with sfdata9ers' Composite OL Ranking and EPA drop-cost cuts — different posts from the video's five); whether the standing daily X sweep cron bodies could absorb the watchlist pass without dropping feed coverage was not verified (applied to the overnight runs first, daily bodies updated the same night); the audio track is silent — nothing missed there.

### S.4 — `~/workspace/your_files/GSE-DESIGN-VS-CODE-GAP-AUDIT-2026-10-23.md` (2026-10-23) — design/content-relevant findings

Scope note: the source is a full design-vs-code audit of the engine (Garrett's question: the shipped engine is "nowhere near what I've designed the past 12 months"). This subsection extracts only the findings that bear on published content — the reasoning copy on pick cards, the narrative layer, and the trust grammar. Grades use the audit's own scale (NOT BUILT / BUILT-DISCONNECTED / BUILT-DRIFTED / WIRED-VERIFIED); code evidence is at commit `e94154fb21` unless noted.

- **Reasoning-trail doctrine (Garrett, 2026-10-23, final form — the content standard):** THE PRODUCT IS THE REASONING THROUGH ALL SIGNALS. Every signal passes through ONE reasoning; the published trail is that reasoning's account of all of it — what it weighed, what it discounted, and why. The market is one small input at its proper small weight, never the headline "Why." Nothing computed sits outside the reasoning; nothing sits idle or half-wired. Companion rule, same day: "wired" means the output is verified in the published artifact.
- **What the published pick's reasoning actually is (audit §2.1):** the reasoning sentences in `packages/prediction-engine/src/scoring.ts` (L1027/1044 spread, L1309 total, L1698 moneyline) assemble market-first — selection + line + book count, a fair-value clause, Edge %, context phrases fired by thresholds on aggregate scores (e.g., "biophysical injury advantage" when injuryScore > 1), a falsifier note, and "Confidence: N/100." `reasoningShort` = consensus clause + first context clause. No scheme, no personnel matchup, no coach, no spot narrative appears in any published sentence, because none is an input to the file. The published probability is 85% market by construction (in-code comment at the factorBreakdown block). The confidence sum's football content is exactly two of fifteen terms (injuryScore, ngsTrackingScore), both arriving as pre-collapsed scalars — the injured players' names are stripped upstream in `game-context.ts`, whose factor descriptions are generic templates.
- **The narrative module drift (audit §2.3):** `packages/prediction-engine/src/engine/factor-narrative.ts` (branch, 4,085 B) — `narrateFactors()` narrates any factor with impact ≠ neutral and |weight| ≥ 5, sorted by |weight|, capped at 3; the module carries no family classification, so on real picks the heaviest factors narrate first — Bookmaker Consensus (weight 30) and Line Movement (weight 10) — and its own header presents "Bookmaker Consensus, weight 30, 100% of bookmakers align on this side" as its example of "the actual reasoning behind the equations." `factorNarrative` / `factorHeadline` occur 0 times in the shipped scoring.ts at the audited commit (the module was not imported). The 2026-10-23 commit titled "wire factor narrative into all three reasoning sites" (branch head c8ee274) shipped scoring.ts as an **11-byte file containing "PLACEHOLDER"**; the prepared wired file never landed; the file was restored at 4142d46, verified blob-identical to the e94154 content (84,140 B). `watchedSignals()` in the same module collapses the weight-0 shadow families to a name list ("Also watching (no weight yet): Pace, Officials, Venue Environment").
- **Narrative & incentive layer (audit §§1.2.15, 2.5):** the design ranks narrative/incentive factors contract incentives > record chases > revenge games > birthdays, backtest-or-cut, tracked in `docs/narrative-tracker/TRACKER.md` under a strict entry schema (entity, kind, game, checkable details, source, provenance, status, dedupe_key; unverified entries never feed the gate). Code reality: the tracker holds **5 entries, all dated 2026-09-13** (Mayfield milestones ×2, Mayfield contract extension, Chase record chase, +1), every one zero-weight "until it survives historical backtesting"; no backtest exists in the record, no code path reads the tracker into scoring, and no entry was added in the five weeks after Sep 13. Gap class: BUILT-DISCONNECTED.
- **Conviction gate (audit §§1.2.16, 2.5):** designed as a second, WITHHOLD-only gate after the engine decides — 7 outside-game signals (rest-travel, market-movement, book-agreement, beat-report, prop-alignment, scheme-matchup, narrative-incentive). Measured live state per the repo AGENTS.md's own inventory: only `book-agreement` is live; `market-movement` dead (line archive stopped 2026-08-22); `beat-report` inert (the news wire is fictional sample data); `prop-alignment` inert pending EVENT_ODDS_INGEST_ENABLED; `scheme-matchup` inert (nflverse unreachable); `narrative-incentive` "has no source at all — the module will never infer a narrative on its own." `requireEvidence` defaults FALSE and flipping it is founder-only, so as shipped the gate changes nothing — and by its founding asymmetry it could only ever subtract picks, never add outside-game reasoning to one.
- **The trust grammar (audit §1.4.45, June 2026 governance layer, design record):** a silent-launch site with a C1–C8 gate-flip sequence and six built, unit-tested, dormant engines (Claim Compiler, No-Bet Adversary, Proof Graph, Market Memory, Signal Lineage, Cost Governor). The GSN R&D trust grammar: Trust Receipts on every recommendation; No-Bet as a first-class decision with a reason taxonomy; a Source Quality Ledger; Confidence Decay; Rumor Quarantine; a Market Integrity Flight Recorder — "the key IP is not a model weight — it is the operational grammar." Graded BUILT-DISCONNECTED (none appears in the audited pick path).
- **A narrative observation channel does exist at the adapter level (audit Addendum 2):** `apps/web/lib/intelligence-core/universal-wiring.ts` (484 lines, read in full by the audit) — its `narrativeObservations` adapter maps injuries, depth chart, news headlines (with sentiment), social (sentiment/volume), memory entries, and officials into INJURY_AVAILABILITY / NARRATIVE_SOCIAL observations carrying named facts ("${player} (${position}) — ${status}"). But the file fetches nothing and loads nothing: every adapter fires only on caller-supplied structs, and whether production invokes it with populated or near-empty structs was not established (indirect evidence points to near-empty: the repo's own SIGNAL_COVERAGE_LIVE census counts injury / weather / ngs / ratings / player / pace / officials at **0 picks attached** in the live database — 7 of 13 signal families).
- **Headline tally (whole audit):** NOT BUILT 20 · BUILT-DISCONNECTED 12 · BUILT-DRIFTED 17 · WIRED-VERIFIED 0 — "not one element" of the design's reasoning surface met Garrett's 2026-10-23 standard (output read in the published artifact). The reasoning-trail row itself is graded BUILT-DRIFTED.
- The audit's own open gaps touching this material: repo AGENTS.md (18,548 lines) was full-text searched but only ~250 lines read closely; `apps/web/lib/conviction/` source files were not read directly (the gate was graded from the repo AGENTS.md's own measured inventory); `composition.ts` contents unread (size only); branch artifacts `artifacts/HANDOFF-REASONING.md` and `artifacts/WIRING-INSTRUCTIONS.md` could not be retrieved via the API; nothing was executed in that pass.

### S.6 — `~/workspace/your_files/deepseek-sports-video-research-prompt.md` (symlink → `~/workspace/goals/galaxy-sports-edge-viral-video-operation/files/deepseek-sports-video-research-prompt.md`)

- The file is the mission prompt for **"OPERATION FULL-COURT PRESS"** — a DeepSeek-run parallel research operation on posting sports videos, written for the Galaxy Sports Edge viral video operation. It casts the model as director of a "500-agent parallel research operation," all lanes simultaneous, depth over speed ("A skim is a failure").
- Stated operator context: @GalaxySportsHQ (X), a new NFL-first sports media account with ~1 follower at writing, plus TikTok, YouTube Shorts, and Instagram Reels as distribution; the operator has a real VM, a live browser, API access, and video production tooling; the output "will be executed literally" — exact endpoints, URLs, steps, numbers.
- **The mandate (verbatim substance):** real game footage, commentary-led, transformative clips. "We do NOT do AI-slideshow videos, static graphics standing in for footage, or watermarked reposts." Every recommendation must serve real sports videos that go viral, posted at the right time, from the right accounts, through the right pipes. Non-negotiable honesty rule: report the real enforcement and legal risks even when the news is bad; sycophancy is a failure mode.
- **Citation Integrity Protocol (marked HARD — "VIOLATION VOIDS THE LANE"):** every factual claim carries a real source URL + retrieval date; anything unverifiable is marked [UNVERIFIED] with what would verify it; never invent a URL, article title, court case, statute number, price, or statistic; a fabricated citation is "the single worst outcome of this operation — worse than an empty section"; every finding classified VERIFIED (primary source) / REPORTED (reputable secondary) / UNVERIFIED (inference, labeled, with reasoning); recency rule — prefer sources from the last 12 months, every source stamped with its publication date.
- **Lane 1 — Footage rights & enforcement reality:** the NFL rights chain (league → broadcast partners ESPN/ABC, NBC, CBS, Fox, Amazon, Netflix → NFL Films → teams) and each layer's clip-redistribution rights; NFL enforcement posture per platform (takedown speed, automated vs manual, strikes, termination risk) with real cases; three X-specific claims to confirm/correct/refute from primary sources (no public strike count + permanent suspension possible on first offense for an infringement-dedicated account; deleting a flagged post clears nothing; counter-notices require real identity, federal-court jurisdiction consent, perjury statement); fair use applied to 3–15-second clips with telestration + original voiceover, with real sports-highlight case law or an admission the case law is thin; YouTube Content ID vs manual claiming; TikTok IP tools; Instagram Rights Manager; how House of Highlights / Overtime / Bleacher Report got league deals and whether any documented path exists for a small account (incl. NFL creator programs); NCAA conference-by-conference differences; MLB/NBA aggressiveness comparison; a case file of ≥10 real sports clip accounts (terminated vs thriving, observable format differences); international angle.
- **Lane 2 — Content specs:** a catalog of viral sports-video formats with real examples (account + post URL + view counts where obtainable): raw highlight, telestrated breakdown, mic'd-up, meme edits, stat-overlay, debate/question, prediction-receipt, sideline/reaction, slow-mo anatomy-of-a-play; optimal video length per platform for 2026, with sources; hook engineering (first 1–3 seconds; caption/title formulas; on-screen text conventions); the commentary-led transformative format specified exactly (clip seconds per segment, voiceover-to-footage ratio, telestration density, caption style) as a reproducible editor's spec; original audio vs trending sounds; what NOT to post (watermarked reposts and TikTok's documented suppression of them, static slideshows, text-on-stock-footage, platform-downranked features, with platform statements cited); gambling/picks content policies per platform — what is allowed, what needs labeling, what gets restricted.
- **Lane 3 — Timing:** best posting windows per platform with sources; the game-window strategy (pre-game lead time, in-game real-time, the 0–15-minute post-play window, immediate post-game, next-morning recap) and which window produces the most viral clips; the speed game (how fast viral clips appear after a play, who posts first, the tooling/workflow for sub-60-second turnaround); cadence before suppression/diminishing returns; day-of-week and seasonality for NFL content; US Central kickoff-to-posting window mapping.
- **Lane 4 — Account strategy:** full teardowns (followers, formats, cadence, hook style, growth mechanics, monetization) of @HouseofHighlights, Overtime, Bleacher Report, Barstool Sports, ESPN, the NFL's own accounts, and 3–5 top faceless NFL clip accounts; single brand account vs network of niche accounts; faceless vs personality-led tradeoffs; new-account warm-up protocols per platform (weeks 1–4, limit/shadowban triggers, cold-start boost mechanics — X's is documented); cross-posting deltas per platform (aspect ratio, captions, hashtags, sound, description); profile optimization for a sports picks/video account (bio formulas, link strategy, pinned content).
- **Lane 5 — APIs & automation:** X API 2026 tiers/pricing, video upload endpoints and limits, rate limits, automatable vs app-only; TikTok official posting API status (app review, Login Kit, upload specs); YouTube Data API v3 (upload flow, quota costs, Shorts); Instagram Graph API Content Publishing for Reels (account requirements, specs, limits); third-party schedulers with real API access (Buffer, Hootsuite, Sprout, Metricool — platforms, limits, prices); unofficial automation per platform (detection, account-risk, real ban cases, stated honestly); the clipping pipeline (record → clip → telestrate → caption → publish; desktop + mobile; AI-assisted clipping tools); the scheduling matrix across all four platforms.
- **Lane 6 — Algorithms:** X algorithm 2026 compiled from the open-sourced code + platform statements, with specific claims to verify or correct (reply ≈ 5–20× a like; repost/quote ≈ 5×; copy-link ≈ 20×; video dwell/completion; recency windows; small-account cold-start mechanics; downrank triggers — external links, engagement bait, muted words, spam cadence); TikTok (completion, rewatch, shares, saves, average watch time, the first-200-views test, trending-sound effects, watermarked-repost penalty, account authority vs per-video merit); YouTube Shorts (view velocity, swipe-away rate, Shorts shelf vs subscription feed); Instagram Reels (DM shares, saves, original audio, originality boost/penalty); suppression triggers per platform with sources; sports-specific algorithmic treatment (event spikes, team-affinity, live-event surfaces); the comment/reply game top accounts use to multiply reach.
- **Lane 7 — Money:** revenue streams ranked by accessibility for a new account (X ad-revenue share requirements + realistic payouts; TikTok creator programs; YouTube Shorts monetization requirements + RPMs; Instagram bonuses/gifts); sponsorship thresholds and rates; betting affiliates (FanDuel/DraftKings rev-share reported at 25–40% — how a small publisher actually gets approved, realistic earnings math); Amazon Associates for sports gear; merch, Patreon/memberships, selling picks (what works, platform-policy constraints); the monetization sequence for a 1-follower NFL account with follower/view thresholds per step.
- **Lane 8 — Footage sourcing playbook:** every source of real NFL game footage ranked by speed, quality, and legal/practical risk (league official YouTube, team sites — "the chiefs.com video-hub model" — broadcast partner apps, press conferences, All-22/coach's film, public-domain archives, fan-shot stadium footage); download methods per source (yt-dlp compatibility, extensions, screen-record workflows, quality ceilings); **the 60-second pipeline** specified exactly (live play → published clip; tools, steps, time per step) — named in the prompt as "the single most valuable deliverable in this lane"; telestration tools the top breakdown accounts use (desktop + mobile, free + paid); caption/subtitle tools that handle sports terminology; the zero-upload lane — what league/team official accounts post themselves that can be quote-posted or reacted to without uploading anything.
- **Required output format (strict):** (1) executive summary — the 10 highest-leverage findings, one sentence each, tagged VERIFIED / REPORTED / UNVERIFIED; (2) per-lane dossiers answering EVERY numbered question or marking it unresolvable with what was tried; (3) a 72-hour action plan for a 1-follower NFL account with the Lane-1 legal guardrails baked in; (4) a 90-day scaling plan (growth, cadence ramp, monetization sequencing); (5) a master source list — every URL with publication date and retrieval date, grouped by lane; (6) open questions with the primary source that would resolve each.
- (Record note: this source is the prompt artifact itself. Whether the operation's output dossier was ever produced, and where it lives, was not established in this pass — see §11.6 item 2.)

### S.7 — `~/workspace/your_files/gse-social-media-playbook/gse-social-media-playbook.pdf` — Social Media Playbook v1.0 (Oct 8, 2026)

- Document facts: "Galaxy Sports HQ — Social Media Playbook," version 1.0, dated Oct 8, 2026; 11 pages; subtitle: "The complete runbook for @GalaxySportsHQ — how the intel becomes posts, how the evidence gates every number, and how game night runs from first practice report to final grade." Channels: X / YouTube / TikTok / IG. Owner: Garrett Baxley, Kingwood TX. Lead operator: Motif. Standing rule printed on the cover: "nothing posts without Garrett's explicit approval." Internal document — pricing, methodology, and data sources stay behind the fence. Production note: the PDF is a HeadlessChrome/Skia print (created Oct 8, 2026) of the HTML in the same folder's `.src/` (OUTLINE.md + index.html + cover media); text for this pass was extracted with Poppler `pdftotext` (no pdfplumber/PyPDF2/PyMuPDF/markitdown exists on this VM — §11.6 item 1).
- **§1 — How to use this playbook tonight:** one operator can run the entire NFL content operation from this document alone — no engine, no extra tools, no second person. The operation has three layers: the intel corpus (SiriusXM transcripts, YouTube sweeps, practice reports, line tracking) gathers what happened; the evidence ladder decides what is solid enough to say out loud; the voice decides how it sounds; a post ships only when all three layers agree. The one-sentence doctrine: "Publish only what a Tier 1 or Tier 2 source (or a corroborated Tier 3 source) supports, say it the way Garrett would say it to a friend, and grade every call in public after the game — win or lose." Tonight, in order: (1) master brief first — read the consolidated brief (last night + today) before drafting anything; conflicts between sources stay visible in the brief's conflicts register, never silently resolved; (2) game-night check at T-minus 75 minutes — inactives, late scratches, roof status, line move since the brief; anything that changes a number is re-checked against the ladder; (3) draft, gate, approve — every draft passes the voice gate and the evidence gate, then waits for Garrett's word, "No exceptions, including replies"; (4) live watch — one post per genuine turning point, not per drive; the learning-loop note is written before kickoff (what we expect, quarter by quarter) so the post-game grade is honest; (5) grade at final — result post within 30 minutes of the final whistle: what hit, what missed, the record in units; misses get the same prominence as wins. Engine status, stated plainly in the playbook itself: as of Oct 8, 2026 the prediction engine cannot produce a verified play (database unreachable, pick worker unbuilt, nothing calibrated); tonight's calls run on the intel corpus and are labeled that way (§9 below); "This playbook never dresses intel up as model output."
- **§2 — The voice that wins (Garrett-human, not optimized):** the account's early posts were "accurate and boring — optimized shapes with no human in them"; the fix is Garrett's own voice studied from his Facebook timeline (2020–2026). The ten voice rules: (1) talk TO the reader — open like a greeting, not a thesis ("Alright, Sunday." / "Okay, real talk."); (2) one vivid image per post — the image does the work ("a refrigerator with the temperament of a toddler"); adjectives never do a noun's job (no "massive," "insane," "elite"); (3) staccato bursts — short declarative sentences, fragments fine, then stop; (4) dry understatement — let the reader laugh, never explain the joke or punch down; (5) blunt one-liners inside the professional floor; (6) questions that answer themselves ("How do you block Hutchinson with that line? You don't."); (7) honest stakes ("Every one posted before kick. Every result posted after, win or lose."); (8) minimal captions — "This." / "Relatable." — over-explaining is the machine tell; (9) country-direct register — "man" and "dude" as seasoning, never the meal, never corporate; (10) player-first lens — Garrett played the game; respect the grind, love the dramatics. The professional floor (never broken): numbers stay in the post; the work stays visible; no cursing; no financial-advice framing — results in units, never dollars ($5 = 1 unit); no injury jokes; no trolling; no hashtags in replies; no em dashes. Banned AI tells: delve, unlock, game-changer, "let that sink in," "nobody is talking about." The slogan "WE DETECT. YOU DECIDE." is suspended — website background only, never in a post. **The read-aloud test outranks every other rule in the section:** read the draft out loud; if it sounds like Garrett telling a friend about the game, ship it to the approval desk; if it sounds like a press release or a betting tout, rewrite it.
- **§3 — Six evidence tiers gate every number** (from the shadow decision architecture adopted Oct 8, 2026): **Tier 1** — official league injury reports, official game books, verified on-site personnel: primary evidence, may move a published number on its own. **Tier 2** — licensed tracking/contract feeds, official league syndication (nflverse/nflreadr class): may move a published number on its own, within license terms. **Tier 3** — credentialed beat reporters, recognized correspondents: evidence only with independent corroboration (a second Tier 1–3 source); uncorroborated Tier 3 goes to the library, not the feed. **Tier 4** — market line movement, betting splits, odds drift: context only; a line move is never proof of sharp action or value; report the move, never cite it as the reason for a play. **Tier 5** — social aggregators, forums, unaccredited communities: never a rationale; may trigger a verification check, nothing more. **Tier 6** — analyst prose, model summaries, generated text (including AI summaries): never evidence; Tier 6 text cited as a fact is an automatic kill (STOP-10). **Point-in-time discipline:** a fact is usable only if it existed before the lock — every note carries valid time, assertion (ingestion) time, and lock time (kickoff); the rule is mechanical: assertion_time ≤ lock_time, or the note is quarantined to the post-lock audit log. The three leakage traps: (1) syndicators quietly rewrite pre-game inactive lists after kickoff — always join on assertion time, never on current table state; (2) warehouse overwrites destroy audit history — notes are append-only, corrections are new rows; (3) a weather scrape from 11:00 PM cannot describe an 8:15 PM kickoff — the only valid weather is what was asserted at or before lock.
- **§4 — The shadow rule: footnote, never headline:** situational adjustments (QB change, wind, short week) are computed, footnoted, scored against reality, and forbidden from touching the published number until promoted. Measured effects table (the playbook's figures): starting QB change, elite → average backup — spread moves 3.5 to 7.0 points (Rosen 2026) — "the one big lever; name it, quantify it"; non-QB starter injury (WR/RB) — under 0.5 points per player, absorbed by depth ("say who is out; invent no point swing"); bye-week rest (post-2011 CBA) — +0.15 points, statistically zero (Lopez & Bliss 2024, 5,260 games) — never sell rest as an edge, already priced; mini-bye / short week — +0.08, not significant (color, not a play driver); high wind (15+ mph sustained) — totals drop 1.8 to 2.8 points (Borghesi 2008) — post the wind number, not a vibe; dome = no weather post; extreme cold without wind — 0.8 points or less on totals (mention only with the actual temperature attached); transcontinental travel, modern era — under 0.4 points, fully priced (story angle only, never an edge claim). **Promotion gates (all four, no exceptions):** (1) sample — 100+ settled games scored out-of-sample from the shadow version's own run; (2) Brier score 0.22 or better; (3) calibration ECE 0.05 or better, Murphy reliability 0.05 or better; (4) stability — three consecutive passing runs, no changes in between. Why the floor is 100 and not 15: with only 13 settled games, even a perfect 13-for-13 certifies a true error rate no better than ~21% at 95% confidence; one miss pushes the bound past 36% — "Small samples can only flatter calibration, never prove it." Any proposal to lower the floor to publish faster is STOP-07 and ends the conversation. Until a shadow adjustment clears all four gates it appears only as a labeled footnote ("our uncalibrated lean, not a play") or not at all: "The published number is the baseline. Always."
- **§5 — The weekly machine (all times CT):** Nightly 11:45 PM — SiriusXM Channel 87 on-demand pull: play the episode first, then open the transcript panel (the button only works once playback starts), scroll to the bottom, extract; priority voices Jeff Mans, Jim Bowden; capture confidence, disagreement, and who analysts trust or fear — not just facts. Wed + Fri 7:30 PM — YouTube sweep (~8 shows each); analyst takes logged to the weekly corpus; duplicates deduped by fuzzy title match. Every 12 hours Thu–Sun — lines and props tracker: multi-book spreads/totals, line movement, ticket-vs-handle splits, prediction-market prices, prop watchlist; snapshots archived; tracker auto-disables after Monday night. Saturday ~8:00 AM — weekly aggregate to Garrett by email, the raw material for his Saturday podcast (his, NotebookLM-assisted; ~3 emails/week total). Sunday 5:00 AM–12:00 PM — live listen (the only live day): Report 1 at ~10:00 AM, Report 2 at 11:30 AM (game-time decisions only), standing authorization to send both. Saturday night — engagement review: per-post metrics pulled, content decisions follow the numbers; standing baseline: single-stat nuggets win, all-caps announcements flop; "Post less, measure more." **The one-note rule:** every situational fact enters the system as one immutable row — note ID, retrieval timestamp (UTC), validity window, game ID (e.g. 2026_05_TB_DAL), player ID, entity confidence, source tier and name, speaker, source URL, the exact verbatim quote, corroboration link (mandatory for Tier 3), situation class, proposed deltas, routing status (LIBRARY_ARCHIVED / SHADOW_ELIGIBLE / ABSTAINED). A note missing its timestamp, tier, or entity link does not exist. Raw transcript text never enters a model as a coefficient — transcripts produce discrete, verified state changes (a status flag flips) or nothing.
- **§6 — Game-night runbook (copy-paste; Thu/Sun/Mon night games; times Central, relative to kickoff):** Pre-game — T-24h: master brief read; learning-loop pre-game note written (what we expect, quarter by quarter, and what would change our mind). T-6h: practice/injury final check; any Tier 1 status change triggers a brief addendum, not a quiet edit. T-75m: game-night check — official inactives, late scratches, roof open/closed, wind (outdoor only), line vs the brief's number; every delta re-tiered. T-60m: drafts written, voice-gated, evidence-gated, queued for Garrett's approval; nothing posts on a timer. T-10m: card post live (approved only); baseline number stated; any shadow lean labeled as a footnote. Live — post on turning points only: scores that swing win probability, injuries to starters, coaching decisions the brief flagged in advance; quote the pre-game note when the game follows it ("we wrote this at 6 PM") and when it breaks it — "Both build trust; only one builds it honestly"; reply lane open with paste-ready replies in Garrett's voice, approval-gated like everything else; mild controversy fine when the numbers disagree with other analysts, cheap shots are not. Post-game — Final +30m: grade post — every call, hit or miss, record updated in units; own misses first if there are any. Same night: learning-loop grade filed against the pre-game note; what the engine of record (even the human one) got right for the wrong reasons gets flagged, not celebrated. Next morning: day brief staged with the grade, the updated record, and the next game's first intel. **Approval gates that never move:** X posts need Garrett's explicit word; publishing anywhere needs his say-so; spending his money needs his cap; everything else in the runbook runs without asking.
- **§7 — Templates that ship (seven shapes, each with a boring-vs-human pair):** (1) **The game card** — human version narrates the card before listing it ("Six plays today, all before kick. Kelce's going to live in the middle of that Raiders zone all afternoon… Card:"). (2) **The primetime scene-setter** — the playbook prints a sample labeled "TONIGHT'S APPROVED DRAFT" (rookie's second career start at Jerry World; Mayfield out 3–6 weeks; Tyler Smith back; CeeDee Lamb off 17 catches for 189; "Dome. Prime Video. No excuses. Who's watching?"). (3) **Breaking news** — human version: "Schefter: [star] is out Sunday. There goes half the survivor pools. Backup SZN starts now." (4) **Owning a miss** — human version: "We had the under. It went over by the second quarter. Our model has been sent to the shadow realm. 0-1. Back tomorrow." (5) **The quiet win** — human version: "We had Kelce over 5.5 before kickoff. He had 6 by halftime. Anyway." (6) **The single-stat nugget** — one number, one image, no preamble; the account's best performer per the Oct 4 baseline ("single-stat nuggets outperform every other shape; all-caps announcements are the worst. When in doubt, post the nugget."). (7) **The reply** — dry humor, light sarcasm, real human; numbers stay; never punch down, never troll an injury, no hashtags; "Brevity is the craft, not a defect."
- **§8 — Guardrails and failure modes (the stop list, STOP-01…STOP-10):** STOP-01 — note has no verified UTC timestamp → reject the note (an untimed fact cannot be proven to exist before lock). STOP-02 — fact asserted after kickoff → quarantine to the audit log; it can never justify a pre-game claim. STOP-03 — ambiguous player/team match → refuse the link ("Wrong-player posts are the fastest trust-killer in the niche"). STOP-04 — source is Tier 4, 5, or 6 → library only; market moves and analyst prose never rationale a play. STOP-05 — Tier 3 without corroboration → block the delta ("One beat writer is a rumor; two are a report"). STOP-06 — raw prose aimed at a model or a post → halt; narrative becomes a discrete verified claim first, or it becomes nothing. STOP-07 — publish attempt below N=100 settled → force lock ("Small samples flatter; they never certify"). STOP-08 — live metric breaches Brier/ECE gates → re-quarantine to shadow; "the footnote lane is always open." STOP-09 — fails 3-run stability → reset the counter to zero ("Consistency is the product"). STOP-10 — claim has no verifiable URL and date → discard ("Unverifiable means unpublished, however good it sounds"). Any trigger halts the post, not the night. **Standing brand guardrails:** public surfaces show projections and rankings only — underlying data, metrics, signals, and methodology stay internal, including all Next Gen Stats material ("reasoning fuel, never published, never named"); results are posted in units, never dollars; no "lock," no "can't miss," no certainty framing — every pick carries a stated probability and its uncertainty; the site never claims calibrated confidence the operation does not have — that copy is flagged for scrubbing as of this writing (see §11.6 item 3).
- **§9 — The engine truth (as of Oct 8, 2026; "three independent probes, all fail-closed"):** Prediction database (Neon) — unreachable from the operator environment; the credential was rotated after an October exposure and the replacement is Garrett's console tap, still owed; even "what is in the database" cannot currently be answered. Pick worker — a stub; no runnable prediction path exists; the only live computation is market de-vigging, "which is arithmetic, not a model." Calibration — nothing is calibrated; the only measured Brier scores on record are worse than a coin flip, both on unmerged branches; the adjustment layer's own header marks every magnitude `calibrated: false`. Public claims — the website still advertises "calibrated betting confidence"; that is an overclaim against this table and is queued for a copy scrub. What it means for the feed: until §10 lands, every play the account posts is an intel-corpus play — sourced, tiered, and reasoned, but not model output; posts say so when it matters and never borrow the engine's authority; "An honest 'nothing verified tonight' beats a fabricated yes, including under deadline pressure. Especially under deadline pressure."
- **§10 — Path to engine-powered picks:** Step 0 — Garrett's two Neon taps (credential rotation confirmed, access restored; nothing downstream can be verified without database access; the only step that needs his hands). Step 1 — situational_notes table (17-field schema, default-deny). Step 2 — market de-vig as baseline v0 (y_pub): the architecture explicitly allows closing consensus as the unconditioned baseline, so real deltas can be shadow-scored against it immediately. Step 3 — shadow consumer live (footnote lane only): starts the 100-game clock; "every week of delay is a week added to the earliest honest publish date." Step 4 — walk-forward backtest: train 2022–2024, validate 2025, live-check 2026. Step 5 — promotion review at the four gates (Brier, ECE, reliability, 3-run stability decide — "Not enthusiasm, not a hot streak"). The realistic timeline is days of build plus one season-fraction of shadow scoring. When the first engine-powered play does publish, it will carry its sample size and calibration state in the post: "That transparency is the brand: the work, visible."
- Playbook's own source list: the voice brief "Making @GalaxySportsHQ Sound Like Garrett" (2026-10-04, Facebook 2020–2026 study); the shadow decision architecture adopted 2026-10-08 (evidence tiers, bitemporal joins, shadow consumer gates); Lopez & Bliss (2024) on rest differential; Borghesi (2007, 2008) on weather and totals; Boulier & Stekler (2003) on forecast benchmarks; Rosen (2026) on quarterback spillovers; the account's own engagement baseline (2026-10-04). The playbook notes several 2026-dated citations in the architecture document are under verification review as of writing and are used as method, not as settled literature.

What it is (verified in the register): a self-hosted, you-owned AI companion runtime aiming to recreate Neuro-sama — Live2D + VRM avatar stage, voice interaction, RAG memory system with embedded database, plugin system (WIP), desktop app with native CUDA/Metal inference (HF candle) plus a browser/PWA version; the character can join Discord voice channels and play Minecraft/Factorio. A dedicated @proj-airi org holds sub-projects (memory, Live2D utilities). MIT, 50,853★, 5,127 forks, TypeScript, created Dec 2024, pushed Oct 28, 2026 — large and very active. Fit / lane: Nova persona RUNTIME candidate — for a future where Nova is live and interactive (streaming/chat), not for produced video; its avatar layer is Live2D/VRM (illustrated models), so it does not touch her photoreal launch path; stronger runtime candidate than eliza (S17) for a live character; both stay WATCH until a live-persona build is ordered. Status: CATALOGED / WATCH (dissected). Wake condition: Nova (or another persona) goes live/interactive.

**S55 — Statline (statlineplay.com) — daily player-guessing game.** Source: https://statlineplay.com. Shared Oct 29, 2026, X For You PM sweep (post by @410Beck); verified in the Oct 30 catch-up pass. What it is (verified Oct 30, 2026 at the product's own site, live): a daily guessing game — one mystery NFL player's verified season statline, four guesses to name him, Wordle-style per-guess feedback (team / height / division / age: match, close, no-match, direction arrows). Skill positions only (QB/RB/WR/TE), fresh puzzle Monday–Saturday, same puzzle for everyone, next drop midnight ET. Fit / lane: Content OS audience mechanics for @GalaxySportsHQ — the format is the find: a statline-guessing ritual teases expertise while being structurally incapable of leaking a pick (fits the tease-only public doctrine). A GSE version must be its own game/presentation, not a clone of Statline's page or feedback design. Status: DISSECTED — LEARN-ONLY (format filed; one pilot post is the proposed test if the lane wants it).
